# Fernlet File Index

This index maps the main project files to their responsibilities. It is intended as a quick orientation guide for app navigation, feature work, tests, and documentation.

Last updated: 2026-10-17 (the eight defects from the anomaly re-triage: the intimacy Health caveat, a pasted recipe queued for tomorrow, the guided intent's reconcile notification, written exercise lines reopening as their rows, fractional durations, the meal-photo folder healing itself, the unrecoverable wall sweeping its files, and the dual-column servings count; Paths are written from the directory containing the repo checkout, so `Fernlet/…` is the repo root: `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/…` is the app target folder, `Fernlet/FernletKit/…` is the local Swift package, and `Fernlet/Tests/FernletTests/…` etc. are the sibling test/extension targets.

## FernletKit Package (Module Map)

The on-device source is carved into the `FernletKit` local SPM package (see [SPM-Module-Carveup-Plan.md](SPM-Module-Carveup-Plan.md)). The app links one umbrella product; the layered dependency DAG *is* the S3 privacy wall — the walled `AIProviders` and `CloudKitSync` modules list no `Private*` dependency, so a forbidden `import` is a hard build error. Per-file rows appear in the feature sections below; this table is the module-level map.

| Module | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Package.swift` | Package manifest: the layered target DAG, per-target MainActor isolation choices, and the two dependencies from outside this repository: CryptoSwift (up to next minor from 1.10.0, for FernletLock's Scrypt KDF) and, since ProximityKit plan step A1.3, ProximityKit (`https://github.com/bowman-mw/proximitykit`, exactly `0.1.1` since the re-pin of 2026-10-17), whose `ProximityKit` product FernletProximityUI, FernletConnections and FernletSocial depend on (`.product(name: "ProximityKit", package: "proximitykit")`); the app project pins it the same way, and `CIGateSelectorBoundaryTests` holds both `Package.resolved` files to one revision. Platforms iOS 26 and, since ProximityKit plan step A0.6.5, macOS 26 (declared package-wide, as SwiftPM declares platforms; the Mac-ready set, FernletFoundation, FernletCrypto and FernletDomainModel, builds for it through `Scripts/macos-build-check.sh`). Products: the `FernletKit` umbrella the app and its tests link (beside ProximityKit's own product, which an umbrella of this package's targets cannot carry), `FernletExchange` for the extensions, and `MacReady` (A1.0.1), which names the Mac-ready set's three modules so the macOS check builds each by name; no app target links it. The in-tree `ProximityKit` target, its product and its package test target, `ProximityKitTests` (A0.8.2), left with the package at A1.3. |
| `FernletFoundation` | Layer 0 — shared utilities: dates, keychain helpers, audit log, storage preferences, monotonic clock, startup timing, backup exclusion. |
| `FernletCrypto` | Layer 0 — pure sealing primitives (`ColumnCrypto`, `DeviceBindingID`, CryptoKit-only) and `CryptographicPurpose` / `FernletCryptoPurpose`, the registry of the app's own cryptographic domains (81). Since ProximityKit plan step A0.2 ProximityKit reads its 39 protocol labels from its own namespace, not from here; 38 registry entries are their twins, and two more (the heart and presence pair salts) twin the feature salts `.fernlet` declares, all 40 pinned equal by `ProximityNamespaceGoldenTests` until they retire (plan step C1), and ProximityKit names no registry entry and, since plan step A1.0, imports none of this module (its identity signs only under its own `ProximityCryptographicPurpose`); 6 more entries twin the signature labels Fernlet mints with ProximityKit's `featureSignature(_:framing:)` (`FernletSocial`'s activity join token, roster snapshot and moderation report, the app's duress request and reply and DEBUG probe introduction), held to the same accepted bytes by `ProximityNamespaceGoldenTests` until C1; the app names it for the sealed-backup escrow's two (`SealedBackupEscrowKey`), and `FernletSocial` for the heart dead-drop's labels, presence's epoch-tag prefix, the ban evidence's reporter tag and the activities' and the moderation report's four. See [Crypto-Domain-Separation.md](Crypto-Domain-Separation.md). |
| `WebScrapingKit` | Layer 0 — zero-dependency web-scraping substrate: shared HTML/JSON-LD helpers for both web importers, plus `EphemeralWebSession`, the no-cookie/no-cache/no-credential "private tab" URLSession every outbound fetch goes through. |
| `FernletDomainModel` | Layer 1 — portable, nonisolated domain value types (nutrition/workout/wellbeing/companion models, settings aggregate, wire DTOs). |
| `FernletScoring` | Layer 1 — scoring/goal-weight/meal-parse/workout-plan engines, abstract period-scoring signal types, and the stress engine. |
| `FoodCatalog` | Layer 1 — USDA food search/scoring; owns the bundled read-only `FoodCatalog.sqlite` resource (loaded via `Bundle.module`). |
| `FernletPersistence` | Layer 2 — persistence contract: `FernletRepository` protocol, `FernletSnapshot` aggregate, and the per-store repositoring protocols. |
| `LocalPersistence` | Layer 2 — Foundation-only local JSON repository, log-record DTOs, and the derived-signal / Tier-2 memory engines. |
| `PrivateStoreCore` | Layer 2.5 — sealed local-only Core Data stack shared by the `Private*` stores (protected side of the S3 wall). |
| `PrivateHealthStore` | Layer 3 — sealed cycle/intimacy store: `PeriodTrackerStore` (sealed `CycleRecord`s are the cycle history's source of truth; Apple Health an optional mirror), the legacy import, cycle prediction, cycle-record, legacy menstrual-narrative and intimacy repositories, raw cycle types. |
| `PrivateMemoryStore` | Layer 3 — sealed journal and worry-box narrative repositories. |
| `PrivateMediaStore` | Layer 3 — at-rest AES-GCM-sealed photo stores (mesh friend photos, meal photos, progress photos). |
| `PeriodContextBridge` | Layer 4 — sanctioned egress converting raw cycle data into the abstract period signals scoring consumes. |
| `AIContext` | Layer 4 — typed AI context payloads (the de-identification contract), the `MemoryAgent` gatekeeper, and the AI audit log. |
| `AIProviders` | Layer 5 — **walled** on-device Foundation-model consumers; structurally cannot import any `Private*` store. |
| `CloudKitSync` | Layer 6 — **walled** iCloud-synced Core Data + CloudKit persistence; must never name a sealed type. |
| `StoreCore` | Layer 7 — store-side services lifted out of the app: derived signals, snapshot saves, AI retry queue, saved recipes, coins/custom items/milestones. |
| `DiaryStore` | Layer 8 — the portable `@Observable` diary slice of `FernletStore` (days, meals, journals, goals, scoring methods); the app-side facade forwards to it. |
| `HealthKitGateway` | Layer 6 — HealthKit platform shim: `HealthKitService`, workout sync, activity-type catalog, authorization seams. |
| `FernletLock` | Layer 6 — app-lock service (Scrypt via CryptoSwift); drains the sealed pending-narrative buffer on unlock. |
| `AppServices` | Layer 6 — assorted platform services: notifications, WeatherKit, nutrition-label OCR, barcode scanning, food image classification, share-extension import queue. |
| `ProximityKit` | The peer-to-peer subsystem (mesh transport, identity, trust, ranging, the presence engine, the routed mesh engine with the public seams its host's features plug into, and the one-to-one pair session Fernlet's recipe share runs over), a package of its own since ProximityKit plan step A1.3: Fernlet depends on [bowman-mw/proximitykit](https://github.com/bowman-mw/proximitykit) at exactly `0.1.1` (the annotated tag on `e09affe4`, since the re-pin of 2026-10-17; `0.1.0`, on `6648094f`, from A1.3), its history carried from this repository from the carve-up on. It depends on nothing; it reads its protocol identity (`ProximityNamespace`, Fernlet's `.fernlet` supplied by FernletConnections), its install binding, its audit sink and every platform fact from its host (`ProximityHost`, the app's `ProximityHostAdapter`). Its sources, its DocC landing page, its package tests and its walls (namespace, platform, keychain door, hygiene, tree, source and memory-lifecycle) are its repository's, built and tested by its own CI on macOS and iOS; Fernlet's strict build compiles the tag with warnings as errors, and FernletTests reaches its internals through `@testable import ProximityKit` (plan step A1.4). Its files were indexed here until A1.3. |
| `FernletProximityUI` | Layer 6.5 — the in-person review screens on top of `ProximityKit`: the session-end photo review with its add-only Photos saver and shared save-failure alert, the keep-as-friend prompt, and `FingerprintText`; owns their copy vault and string catalog. Kept apart from `FernletUI` so the design system carries no networking or Photos dependency ([Plan-FernletCoach-ProximityKit-2026-10-01.md](Plan-FernletCoach-ProximityKit-2026-10-01.md) step A0.1). Depends on `FernletSocial` for the review's `SessionPhotoAnswerFailure`, and on `FernletConnections` for `ProximityNamespace.fernlet`, which both screens hand `PeerNameDisplay`, and for the name placeholders they render through (`PeerNameDisplay.shown`). |
| `FernletConnections` | Layer 6.5 — Fernlet's connection rules on top of `ProximityKit`'s mechanisms (owner decision O3), depending on `ProximityKit` and never the reverse, so ProximityKit can never name Fernlet's identity. Since plan step A0.2.2 it holds `ProximityNamespace.fernlet`, Fernlet's protocol identity (the 39 labels and the two feature salts its family declares, `FeaturePurposes.fernlet` with `FernletFeaturePurposes`, the radios, the QR scheme, the keychain rows, the storage names and the log subsystem, and since A0.3 the app's peer-name policy, `PeerNames.fernlet`: the 24-character cap read off `ItemNameModeration.maxNameLength` and the "A friend" floor), byte-identical to today's literals and sound, which ProximityKit checks at run time before an identity provisions or a radio starts; since A0.2.9 it also holds `FernletDeviceBindingAdapter`, Fernlet's install binding for ProximityKit's column seal (hence its `FernletCrypto` dependency), and since A0.2.10 `FernletAuditBridge`, the sink that sends ProximityKit's audit lines to `FernletAuditLog` (hence its `FernletFoundation` dependency); since A0.3 `ProximityNamespace.Vocabulary.fernlet`, Fernlet's payload vocabulary, which `Family.fernlet` carries beside the radios' three presentation strings (which ProximityKit's radios and name display read off it, as it reads every vocabulary group: its envelope the payload rules, its coordinator the session messages and capabilities, its managers the wire2 token, its inventory digest the record kinds, its routed type registry the routed types and its mesh manager the mesh messages), its payload and capability tokens, its membership record kinds and its mesh messages read off `PayloadType` and `ProximityCapability` (hence its `FernletDomainModel` dependency) and its routed types spelled there alone; and Fernlet's session rules: `FriendSessionTrustPolicy` (what the app's `ProximityHost` adapter hands ProximityKit for each connection), `CoachSessionTrustPolicy` with `CoachSessionContract`, `FriendMintingReview` and the trainer export body, `TrainerExportPayload` (the policies and the review read `FernletDomainModel`'s `TrainerAuditEvent`, `ProximityMode` and `ProximityTrustedPeerRecord`), with `TrainerAuditEvent.init(_:)`, the one conversion from the `ProximitySessionAudit` ProximityKit's coordinator reports to Fernlet's persisted audit row, and `ProximityMode.init(_:)`, the one from its `ProximitySessionMode` to the persisted mode; `ProximityTrustVault`, Fernlet's trusted-peer records and audit rows, which answers ProximityKit's `ProximityTrustStore`; and the name placeholders, its extension of ProximityKit's `PeerNameDisplay` (`Placeholder`, `shown`, `firstName`, `text(for:)`, over the identifier filter that stays ProximityKit's), whose two phrases resolve against the module's own `Localizable.xcstrings` through `FernletConnectionsCopy`; since A0.7.3 Fernlet's recipe profile for ProximityKit's pair session, `ProximityPairProfile.recipeShare(in:)` ([Plan-FernletCoach-ProximityKit-2026-10-01.md](Plan-FernletCoach-ProximityKit-2026-10-01.md) §3.2); and since A0.7.4 Fernlet's typed view of the signed envelope's payload token (`payloadType`, `isUnknownPayloadType`, a typed initializer and a typed `signed`, a `nonisolated` extension over ProximityKit's raw token, `ProximityIdentityEnvelope+PayloadType.swift`), which Fernlet's features and the tests read and sign through. |
| `FernletSocial` | Layer 6.5 — Fernlet's social features on top of `ProximityKit`'s mechanisms ([Plan-FernletCoach-ProximityKit-2026-10-01.md](Plan-FernletCoach-ProximityKit-2026-10-01.md) steps A0.4 and A0.5), depending on `ProximityKit` and never the reverse: the mesh features and the composition that owns the routed mesh engine they ride (`FernletMeshFeatures` over the `FernletSocialHost` refinement: the session photos and their review, live-session chat, the in-session hearts with the heart ledger, the clothing shop, Group Activities, the moderation report relay, friend-state exchange, the capability list, the vouch list, the content layer, and `FernletRoutedTypes`, the three routed-type rows the host hands the engine), presence (`PresenceManager`, which drives ProximityKit's presence radio and delivers in-person hearts, and the identity's presence pair secret and epoch tag), the heart dead-drop (`HeartDropService` with its sealer, prekey store, outbox, dedup store, peer-bundle cache, sidecar seal, storage scope and format census, the identity's heart-drop derivations and the mesh stores' services derived beside the heart-drop service), moderation's `ModerationLedger`, `ModerationBanStore` and `ModerationContentHash`, the `ClosenessLedger`, the `FriendStateCache` with its `CachedFriendState` rows, the parked `TempMessagePayload`, and recipe sharing (`ProximityRecipeShareManager` over ProximityKit's pair session, its diagnostics ring and the recipe payload's wire types, since A0.7.3), with every wire, keychain and on-disk byte the one Fernlet shipped (`FernletFeatureGoldenTests`), and none of their types declared, extended or aliased in ProximityKit (`ProximityNamespaceBoundaryTests`). Deps: `ProximityKit` (`IdentityService` with its fingerprint, pair-secret door, static key agreement, signing and verification under host labels minted with `featureSignature(_:framing:)` and presence epoch clock, the envelope, `ProtectedSidecar`, the prekey bundle, the mesh storage scopes, the mesh engine and its seams with the routed rows' column types, the body framing and the generic content union, for presence the host seam, the coordinator with its public token send and manual commit, ranging, the namespace gate, the observation loop and the peer-name coercion, for recipe sharing the pair session with its profile, typed status and events, peer row and send outcome, and for presence the presence engine, `ProximityPresenceSession`, with the `ProximityPeerChannel` it hands up), `FernletConnections` (`FernletFeaturePurposes`' two declared salts, Fernlet's proximity root, `FernletProximityStorage.productionDirectory` (since ProximityKit plan step A0.6.6), `.fernlet`'s routed types, the name placeholders' `PeerNameDisplay.firstName`, which presence's first name delegates to, the recipe profile, `ProximityPairProfile.recipeShare(in:)`, and the envelope's typed view, which the heart dead-drop signs and reads through and presence, the clothing shop and recipe sharing read through), `FernletCrypto` (the dead-drop's, presence's and the ban store's registry labels, and the activities' and the moderation report's four canonical-bytes domains, the three signed ones signed under their host-label twins, `FernletFeatureSignatures`), `FernletDomainModel` (the value types its stores keep and its mesh features carry, the friend records, the dead-drop's transport seam, and the payload types and capabilities its features register, send and check), `FernletFoundation` (`KeychainItem`, `MonotonicClock`, `FernletAuditLog`, `FernletDate`) and `PrivateMediaStore` (the session photos' wall and pending corpus). No CloudKit or UI edge (the dead-drop's transport is injected by the app); one string catalog, the camera's hold-failure line through `FernletSocialCopy`; MainActor default, the composition, its features and stores and the presence and recipe-share managers main-actor classes. Built for iOS alone: plan step A0.6's native macOS build covers the Mac-ready set (FernletFoundation, FernletCrypto and FernletDomainModel since plan step A1.3; ProximityKit's own repository's CI builds it for macOS), not this module, whose photo path needs UIKit's `UIImage`. |
| `FernletExchange` | Layer 1 — the transport-agnostic exchange core shared by the app and the Messages extension: recipe/workout packets, the `MSMessage.url` envelope, card metadata, and the App Group catalog and hand-off inboxes. Deliberately holds NO repository — it is the only FernletKit product the Messages appex links, and that is what keeps a Messages-hosted process off Fernlet's persistence stack. |

## Localization And The Token/Display Fork

Phase 0 (locale-correctness fixes) and Phase 1 (string-catalog plumbing + the token/display forks)
merged on 2026-08-19; `en` remains the development language and `es`/`fr`/`de` are the planned first
wave (see [Localization-Plan-2026-07-19.md](Localization-Plan-2026-07-19.md)).

| Path | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Localizable.xcstrings` | App-target string catalog. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/InfoPlist.xcstrings` | Localized Info.plist values (permission-purpose strings, display name). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/AppShortcuts.xcstrings` | Localized App Intents / Siri shortcut phrases. |
| `Fernlet/App/FernletWidgets/Localizable.xcstrings`, `Fernlet/App/FernletShareExtension/Localizable.xcstrings` | Extension-target catalogs. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/Localizable.xcstrings`, `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletLockUI/Localizable.xcstrings`, `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletConnections/Localizable.xcstrings`, `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletSocial/Localizable.xcstrings` | Package catalogs (`FernletConnections`' holds the two name placeholders, `proximity.peer.someoneNearby` and `proximity.peer.someoneYouMet`; `FernletSocial`'s the camera's hold-failure line, under its `proximity.camera.` key). A `String(localized:)` inside a package MUST pass `bundle: .module` — without it the lookup resolves against `Bundle.main`, finds nothing, and silently returns the English literal. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/sync-string-catalogs.sh` | Repopulates the catalogs without opening Xcode (`xcodebuild` never writes into an `.xcstrings`; the sync half lives in the IDE, but the same `xcstringstool sync` ships in the toolchain and consumes the `.stringsdata` a build already emits). Run it after adding or changing user-facing strings and commit the catalog diff in the same commit; `--check` for CI. It is additive — keys that disappear are marked stale, never deleted, so a translation is never silently thrown away. |
| `Fernlet/Tests/FernletTests/LocalizationBoundaryTests.swift` | The localization wall (sibling of the S3, no-tracking and Power-of-10 walls). Enforces the rule below. |

**The rule: every string does exactly one job.** A TOKEN is a persisted `rawValue`, a mesh wire byte,
an AI prompt word, a dictionary key, a matching input, an accessibility identifier, or an
export/schema field — tokens are English forever, because they are compared, decoded, signed, and
shipped between processes and devices. DISPLAY is what a person reads, and only display localizes.
Where one string did both jobs it was FORKED: the token keeps its exact characters so already-persisted
data still decodes, and a separate display property sits beside it.

| Forked type | Token (persist / sign / prompt / compare) | Display |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `CareGroup` | `token` | `label` |
| `MealConfidence` | `token`, plus a `legacyTokens` table for the pre-fork English phrases already in users' blobs | `label` |
| `MealType` | `rawValue` — persisted on every meal AND the meal-parsing prompt's vocabulary, round-tripped as `MealType(rawValue:)` | `displayName` |
| `WorkoutType` | `rawValue` — persisted on workout rows, matched by the exercise catalog, part of the trainer export | `displayName` |
| `ExerciseTarget` / `PrescribedExercise` | `name` — the catalog identity, plan and log text, progression key, AI vocabulary and coach-plan wire | `displayName` / `displayLine` through `ExerciseHowToCatalog`; the guided run bakes it into `GuidedWorkoutRunState.Exercise.displayName` for the Live Activity |
| `CompanionState` | `rawValue` — persisted on `DailyHealthScore`, byte-mirrored by `WidgetCompanionState` in a SEPARATE PROCESS, and a Coach-export field | `displayName` |
| `CoachPlanTokens` vocabularies | the token vocabularies the export prompt publishes to plan authors | the app's own copy |
| ProximityKit `PayloadSummary.title` / `subtitle` / `extraDetails` | frozen English — they are written into the canonical signing bytes and rendered in the RECEIVER's Connection Inspector | (none yet; a localized inspector would render `payloadTypeToken` through a receiver-side table) |

Every failure mode here is silent: nothing crashes, no test goes red on its own, and the damage
surfaces as lost rows or wrong behaviour in a language nobody on the team reads. That is why it is a
wall and not a review checklist.

## App Entry And Shell

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletApp.swift` | SwiftUI app entry point. Creates the shared persistence, app store, period tracker, launch preparation service, and lock service objects. Runs the one-shot Phase-6 backup-exclusion launch gate and hosts its one-time existing-install prompt. Since network migration P7 item 2 its six lifecycle edges (the launch mount, the two scene legs, the two protected-data notifications, the duress observer) hand the raw phase and the lock service's two facts to `FernletStore.applyProximityRunPolicy(…)` and decide nothing: the file writes no routed access gate and assembles none (`MeshRoutedLockedDeviceTests` W6–W8). Since ProximityKit plan step A0.2 (the audit sink) `init()` first installs ProximityKit's audit sink, `ProximityAudit.install(FernletAuditBridge())`, unconditionally and before anything can build a ProximityKit object, so the hosted unit tests' `FernletAuditLog` captures see every ProximityKit line (`ProximityAuditBridgeTests`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BackupExclusionLaunchGate.swift` | Security-hardening Phase 6: `FernletPriorUseMarker` (device-local first-run latch) + `BackupExclusionLaunchGate`, the launch-time resolver that defaults fresh installs to backup-excluded, records already-excluded existing installs, and requests the one-time honest trade-off prompt for existing included installs — never a silent flip. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ContentView.swift` | Main app container and navigation shell. Hosts primary tabs, launch state, lock gate integration, quick sheets, and meal/journal notifications. Mirrors `selectedTab` into the store for the run policy (network migration P7 item 2), as it already mirrors `lockState` and `duressSessionActive`, and since **P7 item 3 speaks no radio verb**: the Friends three-way arm, the tab's five-minute timeout, the `hasCommittedPeer`-guarded stand-down and the presence / recipe listener chains are gone from here (`ProximityRunSeams.swift` executes the policy's verdict); the view hands the funnel its tab, lock-state, presence-opt-in, age-record and launch edges through `applyProximityRunPolicyFromView()`, keeps only the away-hearts CloudKit sync on those edges (`syncAwayHeartsIfActive()`), and its scene handler moves no radio — a scene edge is `FernletApp`'s. Since **session photos U3** (2026-09-30) it also hands the funnel the session-photo review's discovery block as a sixth edge (`.onChange(of: store.sessionPhotoReviewCoordinator.blocksDiscovery)`), holds the review's `SessionPhotoReviewTriggers` on `rootSheetHost` (the overlay window's scene, the scene phase, `launcher.isDone`, the root sheet for First Aid) and, in DEBUG, runs `UITestSupport.seedHeldSessionPhotosIfRequested(on:)` after launch preparation. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/FernletUIComponents.swift` | Shared UI primitives: adaptive color tokens, headers, chip styles, sheet fields, section pickers, layout helpers (including the floating tab bar's clearances: `fernletTabBarBottomClearance()` for a tab page's scroll content, `fernletTabBarSafeAreaClearance()` for a pushed page with a pinned save bar, which gives way to the keyboard once it covers the bar; `fernletKeyboardOverlap(_:)` measures how far the keyboard reaches), searching pulse, medallion/coin glyphs. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/FernletPrimitives.swift` | `FernletCard`, `SectionLabel`, `EmptyState` — the cross-screen layout primitives extracted from HomeView for package-resident views. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/FernletTheme.swift` | App-wide color palette, theme defaults, custom light/dark background support, and UIKit/SwiftUI color vending. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/FernletDesignSystem.swift` | Design-system foundation: the `FernletTextRole` serif/sans type scale, warm token palette, 8pt spacing/radii/shadow/motion tokens, and bundled-font resolution. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/ModelColors.swift` | SwiftUI `Color` extensions mapping domain enums (`MealType`, `WorkoutSplit`, `WorkoutType`…) to palette colors; split out so domain models stay Foundation-only. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/FernletFeedback.swift` | `FernletFeedback` — the app's one seam for haptic and sound cues: named `FernletFeedbackEvent`s (rest ended, hold started/ended, set logged, block changed, workout finished, the opt-in zone drift) mapped to a `FernletHaptic` and an optional quiet `FernletSound`, gated by the caller's `FernletFeedbackPreferences`, played through `UIFeedbackGenerator` and AudioServices system sounds (silent-switch aware, never taking the audio session); an injected closure lets tests record what played. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/FernletSpeech.swift` | `FernletSpeaker` — the sibling seam for opt-in spoken cues: says a caller-resolved `FernletSpokenLine` through the VoiceOver announcer while VoiceOver runs (only what the screen has not already said) or aloud through a `FernletSpeechVoice` (production: `FernletSynthesizerVoice`, one lazy `AVSpeechSynthesizer` in the app's language) otherwise; configures the audio session per route (`FernletSpeechAudioPolicy`: `.ambient` on the speaker, `.playback` + `.voicePrompt` + duck + interrupt-spoken-audio in headphones) only while speaking and releases it with `.notifyOthersOnDeactivation`; every session call (`FernletSpeechSessionCall`), the voice, the route and the VoiceOver read are injectable. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/ActivityShareView.swift` | `ActivityShareView` — the app's single `UIActivityViewController` wrapper, with the `onFinish` completion hook the plaintext exports are deleted at. Shared by the data export (`PrivacyDataSettingsView`), the trainer/nutritionist summary (`TrainerExportView`), and the proximity connection-log export (`ConnectionInspectorHistoryView`); replaced the two private copies and one `ShareLink`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/OptionalPresenceBinding.swift` | `Binding.isPresent()` — collapses an optional-backed binding into the `Bool` shape SwiftUI's `alert` / `sheet` / `confirmationDialog` / `fullScreenCover` modifiers take (presented while non-nil; setting `false` clears the value, setting `true` is a no-op). The shared home for the "present while this optional state is set" binding previously inlined at every call site (`ActivitiesView`, `ConnectView`, `DisposableCameraView`, `FriendListView`, `FriendShopView`, `PrivacyDataSettingsView`, `FriendPhotoReviewSheet`); sites with a compound getter or a side-effecting setter deliberately keep their hand-rolled binding. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletUI/CaptureProtection.swift` | Screen-capture **friction** (never a security control — Docs/Design-Capture-Protection-2026-08-10.md): `CaptureProtectionState` (injected `@Observable` owning the screenshot pulse + per-window-scene `isCaptured` verdict, conservative live-scene fallback pre-registration) and `CaptureProtectedModifier` / `captureProtected(surface:)` (opaque Tier-2 cover while recorded/mirrored or scene-inactive, resigning keyboard focus on arrival; Tier-1 blur + once-per-session nudge — copy in `CaptureNudgeCopy`, announced once for VoiceOver — after a screenshot, skipped while the surface is occluded). Applied by the app target at the six Private-tab surfaces: the hub root (inner to the lock gate) and the five sensitive sheets. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletNotificationDelegate.swift` | `UNUserNotificationCenterDelegate` that presents gentle notifications while foregrounded and routes a check-in tap to the journal sheet via a pending-open flag `ContentView` consumes. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/UITestSupport.swift` | DEBUG-only launch hooks for UI tests: central reader of the `FERNLET_UI_TEST_*` appearance-test flags (seed demo, bypass private lock, reset the app lock / seed an unopenable entry for the no-passcode gate, jump-to-screen), plus `isTestHarnessActive` (XCTest/UI-test detection that suppresses the backup-exclusion launch gate under automation, including a launch that sets only ProximityKit's `PROXIMITYKIT_UI_TEST_*` mesh flags). Since session photos U3: `-uitestSeedHeldSessionPhotos <n>` (`seededHeldSessionPhotoCount`, clamped 1...10) and `seedHeldSessionPhotosIfRequested(on:)`, which holds n generated JPEGs through the manager's own capture door with no session live — awaiting in the sealed pending corpus, never on the wall — unless a review is already outstanding, for `SessionPhotoReviewFirstUITests`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/UITestPrivateHubSeed.swift` | DEBUG-only `UITestPrivateHubSeed`: at launch, resets the app lock and optionally seals one cycle note under a throwaway key, so `PrivateTapGateUITests` starts from a known no-passcode key state and can reach the "can't be opened" card. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Assets.xcassets` | Image and color assets used by the app. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Fonts/` | The six bundled font files (Fraunces, DM Serif Display, Instrument Serif ×2, DM Sans ×2), registered by `Info.plist` `UIAppFonts` and resolved by PostScript name through `FernletFontName`. SIL Open Font License 1.1, not Apache-2.0. Playfair Display Italic was dropped on 2026-10-01 (see the FernletUI landing page). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Fonts/LICENSES/` | One `<Family>-OFL.txt` per font family: the copyright notice and OFL 1.1 text that must ship with the fonts. The synced folder copies them into the app bundle root beside the fonts; `FernletFontRegistrationTests` checks every `UIAppFonts` file has its license there. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Info.plist` | App bundle metadata and platform configuration. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Fernlet.entitlements` | App capability entitlements. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SettingsSearchIndex.swift` | `SettingsRoute` (every addressable Settings destination, driving the hub's single `navigationDestination`) and `SettingsSearchIndex` (the searchable entry table). Adding a route is a compile-time prompt to give it a view and at least one search entry. |

| File | Purpose |

| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/HomeView.swift` | Home dashboard with companion state, quick logging, signal trends, macro summaries, hygiene, and photo wall UI. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodView.swift` | Food logging, recipes, imported recipe review, ingredient editing, meal creation, macro display, saved recipe book, and Safari presentation. Also hosts the in-file `RecipeDetailView` (sealed photo, macros, edit/log/share actions, in-app Safari source link). The Food root pushes the recipe book and recent-recipe details as `FoodRoute` path values (pruned when a recipe is deleted; `LastKnownRecipePage` keeps the deleted recipe drawn while its page pops), so a re-tap of the Food tab pops the whole stack — asking first when the pushed recipe editor or import holds typed input. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WebImportEstimateNote.swift` | The line under a web-imported recipe's macros saying how many ingredients its USDA estimate left out ("2 ingredients not counted in this estimate"; ingredient-search round F11) and how many it weighed by a USDA typical size ("2 ingredients weighed by a USDA typical size (estimate)"; F4b fix round 1), on the recipe detail's macros card, the book row and the notes sheet. Renders nothing for a label-sourced or complete estimate. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CustomIngredientEscapeRow.swift` | The "Create custom ingredient" escape shown beneath a recipe ingredient's non-empty catalog matches (ingredient-search round F9); once tapped it reads as the add-macros instruction and the list stays tappable. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MoveView.swift` | Movement/workout screen with workout logging, suggestions, workout rows, and goal summaries. Hosts `WorkoutExerciseDraft`, the exercise-draft state machine shared by `WorkoutSheet` and `WorkoutPlanSheet`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ActivityPickerSection.swift` | Activity-mode workout picker, recent activity shortcuts, and activity-specific workout fields. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/JournalView.swift` | Journal calendar, prompts, entry creation/editing, day detail, day nutrition breakdowns, and daily edit sheets. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MemoryNoteDisplay.swift` | Display fork for Core memory rows: `MemoryNote.displayText` shows a memory's own text, or builds an emotion-only journal memory's sentence from its `FeelingTag` at render time (the token is stored, the sentence never is). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CycleTrackerView.swift` | The Private hub's merged Cycle page: one layered calendar (period flow tints + a distinct intimacy marker), period predictions/trends, and a single plus-menu; each half gates on its own derived visibility. After an unlock: drain → legacy import → load (period-data design 2026-09-30, §9.1), with no Apple Health prompt (Q4); the day's Delete, "Keep in Fernlet" and "Delete from Apple Health" actions with their outcome alerts; the earlier-cycle-notes card (§8.2). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CycleDayDetailView.swift` | Combined day detail for the Cycle calendar: the period half — "Your entry" (Fernlet's sealed records), "From Apple Health · Fernlet" (its copies with no record here, with "Keep in Fernlet" / "Delete from Apple Health" on a Health-only day) and "From Apple Health · <source>" read-only — with Edit/Log this day/Delete, and the intimacy half (events/notes); each half rendered only when its own gate allows. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/LogPeriodSheet.swift` | Sheet for logging or editing a cycle day. Every field saves in Fernlet whatever the Health switches say (the cutover, period-data design 2026-09-30, §6.3): a fresh log through `logEvent`, an edit in place through `editRecord` (seeded losslessly from the record's blocks), an emptied edit through `deleteRecord`. While cycle sharing is off it says so up front (`logPeriod.healthNotice`), maps the store's outcome to one sentence pinned above the Save bar (`outcomeSentence`: held until Private opens, the Health copy failed, an older Health copy removed), names every save error (`errorSentence(for:)`), and refuses "First day of cycle" without a flow level. Owner report 2026-09-29. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MonthCalendarCard.swift` | `MonthGridModel` + `MonthCalendarCard` — the shared month-calendar chrome (paging chevrons with future months disabled, weekday row, 7-column grid inside a `FernletCard`) plus its pure layout math, whose day keys are canonicalized through `FernletDate.dayKey` rather than a locale-following formatter. The single home of the previously duplicated month grids, now filled by `JournalView` and the layered cycle calendar in `CycleTrackerView`; each supplies its own day cells and footer. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PrivateHubView.swift` | Private hub screen with private-section navigation; hands the app's `PrivateHubOpenCoordinator` to its gate so the no-passcode state shows the one-button tap screen. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SocialHubView.swift` | Social hub entry point for the Friends photo wall and active disposable-camera session flow. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SessionHeartStatusCopy.swift` | **P6 item 6**: `SessionHeartStatusCopy`, the app-side fork of `MeshSessionHearts.SessionHeartFailure` (`FernletSocial`'s in-session hearts) into `LocalizedStringKey` copy — one sentence per frozen cause, an exhaustive `switch` so a new cause is a build error until it has a sentence, plus the "Sent …" line. It closes a LIVE hole rather than tidying one: the case it replaced was `failed(message: String)`, composed inside ProximityKit and rendered verbatim in every language — the D-13.15 defect in a seam D-13.15 did not reach, and invisible to both halves of `LocalizationBoundaryTests` (a bare `String` associated value matches neither scanner; five of its six sentences were measured absent from the catalog). `DisposableCameraView` renders this for the mesh path and keeps a separate `String?` property for the presence fallback, deliberately: typing the shared row `LocalizedStringKey` would force `LocalizedStringKey(runtimeString)` on `PresenceManager.heartSendState.failed(message:)` and move the defect instead of closing it (P9's). Since 2026-09-29 the recipient is named through `shownRecipient(_:)` (`PeerNameDisplay`, "Someone you met"), never raw: a friend kept before their name arrived has their fingerprint filed as the name, and both presence builders borrow the same helper for their names. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ProximityRunPolicy.swift` | **P7 item 1** (plan §13 option A): `ProximityRunPolicy`, the app-layer run policy as a pure VALUE — one function from the app's ten lifecycle facts (`ScenePhase`, `FernletTab`, duress, iOS data protection, the final below-minimum-age ruling, a delete-all in flight, P8's `ProximityContinuationState`, and `ProximitySessionPresence` folded from `isInSession` × `hasCommittedPeer`) to a `ProximityRunState` per radio (mesh, discovery, presence, recipe share) plus the `MeshRoutedAccessGate` the app already assembles. Four run states, not §13's three: `hold` names today's committed session in the background without a continuation task (kept, nothing runs, iOS suspends — never torn down) and the same session off-tab, because `stopJoin()` tears the committed slots down too. The one foreground fact is `FernletApp.routedGateForeground(for:)`, so `.inactive` is foreground for radios as well as for the gate. Decides radios, never plaintext: the gate reads no tab, age, wipe or task fact. **Item 2** made it the single writer of `applyRoutedAccessGate(_:now:)` through `FernletStore.applyProximityRunPolicy(…)`, and added the input projections — `appLockEngaged(_:)` (`.locked` only; RETIRED with its fact by P9-3-A's fix, 2026-09-22, so a configured lock no longer parks presence and recipe) and `belowMinimumAge(_:)` (the final `.below` ruling against `AgeGate.chat` or guardian communication limits, never `.undetermined`). **Item 3** made it the single writer of every radio seam too (`ProximityRunSeams.swift`). The matrix over the full 11 520-row input product (23 040 until P9-3-A) is `ProximityRunPolicyTests`. **Session photos U3** (2026-09-30) added the eleventh fact, `sessionPhotoReviewBlocksDiscovery` (`SessionPhotoReviewCoordinator.blocksDiscovery`, which is false over a live session): it lowers a RUNNING discovery verdict to `heldForACommittedPeer(session)`, so neither `startJoin` nor the resume arm runs while an ended session's photos wait (Q4), and it moves no other radio, no gate leg and no verdict that was not running; the product doubled to 23 040 rows. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ProximityRunSeams.swift` | **P7 item 3**: the radios' `apply(_:)` seams and the ONE place in the app that speaks a radio verb. `ProximityRunTransition` is the pure half — last verdict applied, new verdict, the mesh manager's three live predicates (`isSearching`, `isInSession`, `hasCommittedPeer`) plus each listener's own `isListening` → an ordered `[ProximityRunAction]` (`startJoin` / `resumeSearch` / `stopJoin` / `leaveSession` / arm or cancel the tab's five-minute timeout / `holdLinks` / `presence(_:)` / `recipeShare(_:)`) — the mesh radio edge-triggered so `stopSearching()`'s once-per-ending hooks never fire on a tab bounce, the two listeners reconciled against their own `isListening` on every run (P8 item 0 (b): a `didNotStart*` self-stop under an unchanged verdict is re-started at the next policy run, not at the next verdict edge), resolving `FriendsDiscoveryEntry` unmoved, picking `leaveSession()` over `stopJoin()` on a hard stop by `isInSession`, and never reading `isSessionLive`. The executor is a `FernletStore` extension with one `switch`; `PresenceManager.apply(_:)` and `ProximityRecipeShareManager.apply(_:)` are the listeners' seams. The one transition P7 could not execute — discovery `stop` while the mesh keeps `run` — is **P8 item 3's `holdLinks`**: `MeshNetworkManager.holdCommittedLinks()` stops browsing and closes the admission doors while keeping every committed slot, its coordinator and the group-key state, and `stopJoin()` is still never run over it. The refusal, its case and its audit token (`proximityRunPolicy.unsupportedTransition`) are gone, held at zero under `App/` by `ProximityRunSeamsTests`; the way back out is an explicit row in `discoveryStart` — radios down with a peer still committed ⇒ `.resumeSearch`, no timeout armed — because `FriendsDiscoveryEntry` answers that shape `.none` and the hold would otherwise be a one-way door. Replaces `ContentView.startFriendsDiscovery()` / `stopFriendsDiscovery()` / `armDiscoveryTimeout()` and the two listener chains, and `FernletStore`'s three direct listener stops. Walled by `ProximityRunSeamsTests`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ProximitySessionPoller.swift` | **P7 item 4**: the app's half of the poller. `ProximitySessionPoller` is the pure decision — a timer if and only if `isSessionLive` (`decision(isSessionLive:isPolling:)` → `start` / `stop` / `keep`), the 30 s `interval`, and `maxTicks` (the 6-hour ceiling in ticks, the loop's R2 bound). The `FernletStore` extension owns the ONE timer: `syncSessionPoller()` is called by the run-policy core after every edge and reached by `ContentView`'s `isSessionLive` observer between edges; the tick drives `MeshNetworkManager.pollSession(now:)` and stops itself when a poll reports the session gone. Deliberately keyed on liveness, never on a radio verdict. Walled by `ProximitySessionPollerTests`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SessionResumeCopy.swift` | **P7 item 5**: `SessionResumeCopy`, the app-side fork of `MeshNetworkManager.sessionResumePresentation` into `LocalizedStringKey` cards (`SessionResumeCard`: title, one sentence, an SF Symbol name) — one card per presented case, none for `.nothing`, and one shared dismiss label — in `RoutedShareRefusalCopy`'s shape: one place, exhaustive `switch`es so a new presentation or ending is a build error until it has a sentence. Every sentence says what is true and no more: an offer asks the person to keep the tab open (the restore arms no radio), an ending is named as ended and never as failed, the set-aside file says nothing already saved was lost. Seven new catalog keys, owed to the close-out's sync. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MeshContinuationCoordinator.swift` | **P8 item 4** (plan §14): the continued-processing task's lifecycle as a pure VALUE — `MeshContinuationCoordinator.transition(from:on:)` over six `MeshContinuationState`s (`idle` / `requested` / `running` / `refused` / `expired` / `completed`) × eight `MeshContinuationEvent`s (§14's six plus `taskStarted` and `taskRefused`, without which `running` and `refused` are unreachable) → the next state, the `ProximityContinuationState` `ProximityRunPolicy` is fed (derived from the next state, never a second field), the `MeshContinuationCompletion` the system is owed, and a frozen `MeshContinuationAudit` token for item 6 to emit. The invariant iOS enforces is one line: `completion != nil ⟺ (from == .running && next != .running)` — exactly once per ENTRY INTO `running`, which equals once per delivered task under item 6's one registered identifier and is never once per session; the feasibility probe's `completeBackgroundTask(success:)` idiom generalised. A new mesh resets the claim and completes a stale task `false`; a delivered task is adopted from every state; a request the system never ran is `refused`, not `expired`; a request that is WITHDRAWN resets to `idle`, because `completed` absorbs `firstPeerCommitted` and would leave a re-committing peer with no claim for the life of the mesh; a foreground return completes the task `true` and lands back on `requested`, entry into which IS item 6's instruction to submit (the audit token names the event, never the next move). Registers, submits, drives and completes NOTHING itself (no `BackgroundTasks`, no scheduler, no timer, no radio verb, no store, no clock, no log — item 6 owns all of that), and the completion is a signal, never a teardown (§25.1: the production shutdown keeps the tunnel). The 48-row table is `MeshContinuationCoordinatorTests`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MeshContinuationProgress.swift` | **P8 item 4**, the arithmetic half: `MeshContinuationProgress` is the task's progress as a ratcheted fraction of the session ceiling — §14's "progress must advance monotonically or the system kills the task", held twice over (the unit is monotonic elapsed session time, and `advancing(from:elapsed:budget:)` ratchets with `max(previous, computed)`), clamped finite at construction so `completedUnitCount` converts without a trap and capped one short of `totalUnitCount` (100) exactly as the probe's does; a zero, negative or non-finite budget yields no new progress rather than a division. Takes plain `TimeInterval`s — `MeshSessionCeiling`'s `hardDeadline` and `monotonicBudgetSeconds` are internal to ProximityKit, and the widenings belong to items 5/6 — so the file imports nothing but Foundation. Beside it `MeshContinuationCopy` carries the card's two sentences as `LocalizedStringResource` (`Fernlet mesh`, `%lld friends connected`) for item 6 to render with `String(localized:)` at the request site; the friend count is passed in, and is branch PRESENCE (committed active slots, self excluded), a smaller and honester claim than §14's "fresh authenticated heartbeats" — the session holds one `lastExternalHeartbeatAt` for the whole mesh, not one per member. Two catalog keys owed to the close-out's sync — and at that sync `%lld friends connected` also owes a hand-authored `one`/`other` plural block plus a `LocalizationBoundaryTests.pluralRuledKeys` row, which cannot be added before the key reaches the catalog without reddening that wall. Walled by `MeshContinuationProgressTests`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MeshContinuationCardPresentation.swift` | **P8 item 7** (plan §14, §17.3): what a refusal, an expiry and a system end PRESENT. `MeshContinuationCardPresentation.card(state:lastAudit:)` is a pure table over item 4's claim — six `MeshContinuationState`s × the ten audit tokens (nine plus none) → one `MeshContinuationCard` or nil — and `MeshContinuationCardKind` (`refused` / `expired` / `endedBySystem`) is its frozen token, spelled three times over: the suffix of the card's accessibility identifier, the vocabulary of the DEBUG launch hook `FERNLET_MESH_CONTINUATION`, and the test pin. The state alone cannot decide, which is the whole design: `completed` is item 4's terminal after ANY session end, so the six-hour ceiling would erase a refusal the person was never shown — the ending is carried by the last token that MOVED the claim (`refused` / `expired` / `cancelled` survive it, `absorbed` is not a move and falls back to the state's own meaning). A claim that is idle, asked for or running says nothing. Three sentences and no dismiss button: a refusal is not a failure, and the card describes the session running right now — the session stays open while Fernlet is on screen (refused), or it carries on in the foreground with nothing sealed lost (expired / ended by iOS, one shared sentence). Once that session has itself ended (`completed`) the ending keeps its headline and the message folds to one shared PAST-tense sentence, because every present-tense word about "this session" is false there. Six `LocalizedStringKey` keys, owed to the close-out's sync. Two rules the file states for item 6's driver, one of them as code: `projectedAudit(previous:outcome:)` is how the driver assigns `meshContinuationLastAudit` — an absorbed row and the move INTO `completed` keep the previous token, so the six-hour ceiling cannot erase a refusal nobody was shown — and the header owes it the `.cancelled`-for-an-unservable-delivery and hard-stop-reset obligations. Rendered by `ConnectView`'s card slot, which P7's resume card shares: `slotDecision(continuation:resume:)` is the precedence as a value — a live spent claim wins, an ended one yields to a resume card and takes the slot only when there is none. Walled by `MeshContinuationCardPresentationTests` (the 60-cell product, the observed-projection pin and the surface's single read) and `MeshContinuationCardUITests` (tier 1b, the card by identifier). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MeshContinuationDriver.swift` | **P8 item 5** (plan §14, §24.1, §25.1): the app's continuation driver — item 5's SESSION-STATE half. Holds item 4's claim (`MeshContinuationState`, moved through `MeshContinuationCoordinator.transition(from:on:)`) and, on exactly the moves that table makes, speaks the two raises ProximityKit widened for it: `taskDidStart()` adopts a delivered `BGContinuedProcessingTask` and — on the ENTRY into `running`, never on the event — raises `MeshNetworkManager.beginBackgroundContinuation()`; `taskDidEnd(_:)` (expired / cancelled / sessionEnded / appForegrounded) moves the claim and, only when the table produced a completion, records it in `pendingCompletion` for item 6 to take with `consumePendingCompletion()` and raises `endBackgroundContinuation()`. An ending of nothing therefore raises nothing — the one negative that keeps a second code path out of either raise. The PROJECTION is a rule, not a raw token (item 7's verify): `lastAudit` rides beside `state`, because item 4's `completed` is the terminal after ANY session end and the state alone cannot say which ending it was, and it is assigned through `MeshContinuationCardPresentation.projectedAudit(previous:outcome:)` — item 7's rule, in item 7's file, not a second copy — while the audit LOG still records every move including the absorbed ones. `MeshContinuationAdoption` is the caller's instruction for a delivery (`claimed` / `ownerless` / `absorbed`): an ownerless one — item 4's `idle + taskStarted` — is adopted so the handle is completable at all, must be ended AT ONCE with `.cancelled`, and its ending RESETS the projection, because "iOS ended your background session" is a sentence about a session that never existed. `reset()` is that act exposed, for item 6 to wire to the proximity hard stop (delete-all, leg 7b) and to a new mesh start; it never clears `pendingCompletion`, since a wipe does not excuse the app from completing the handle it holds, and — the fix round's F1 — it ENDS a task still in hand first (`taskDidEnd(.cancelled)`, which completes it once and raises the foreground edge once) before the private `resetProjection()` clears the three fields, because a projection cleared out from under a live claim would strand `continuingInBackground` for the life of that mesh with nothing left to raise `.foregrounded` and every routed heart deferring silently behind it. The manager is held **weakly** and a raise that reaches none is audited (`mesh.continuation.raiseWithoutAManager`), never swallowed. **Nothing spins**: no `Task` handle, no timer, no `unowned` host (so ML1/ML4 do not apply) and — until item 6 — no `BackgroundTasks` import at all; it writes no routed access gate, calls no store setter and speaks no radio verb, which `MeshContinuationRaiseWallTests.theDriverIsSessionStateOnly` pins by needle. Item 6 added the remaining claim edges (`meshDidStart()`, `firstPeerDidCommit()`, `taskWasRefused()`, all through one private `offer(_:)` branch) and put the scheduler half in a SIBLING, `MeshContinuationTaskHost`, which owns one of these — so `theDriverIsSessionStateOnly`'s needle list was not shortened by three and every `BackgroundTasks` name stays forbidden here permanently. The manager is held as `ProximityKit.MeshContinuationRaising`, the two-verb seam it conforms to — a `final class` cannot be stood in for, so counting the raises needs a protocol, and behind two verbs the rest of the manager is unspellable from here. Walled by `MeshContinuationRaiseWallTests` (the three counts), `MeshContinuationDriverTests` (the claim arithmetic, the raises over a session-less manager, and the exhaustive sweep: every sequence of driver calls to depth three over item 6's eleven-wide alphabet (the scene is part of the walk), 1463 walks (eleven-wide, depth 3), one begin per entry into running-while-dark and one end per dark exit — and `claimed` reachable at last, because `firstPeerCommitted` is the edge that breaks the circle) and `MeshContinuationDisagreementTests` (plan §24.1's cell on a real founding, plus the mid-task reset and the ownerless round trip on a live session). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MeshContinuationTaskHost.swift` | **P8 item 6** (plan §14, §25.1): the wiring — the thin object that owns the mesh's `BGContinuedProcessingTask`. Registers the CONCRETE identifier `MBO.Fernlet.mesh-continuation.<meshID>` when a mesh starts (inside `Info.plist`'s `BGTaskSchedulerPermittedIdentifiers` wildcard; a `UUID`'s characters are legal in a task identifier), submits with `.fail` on the ENTRY into item 4's `requested` — which is both the first committed peer and the re-arm a foreground return produces, so the two edges share one test and a founding that commits in the same transaction cannot submit twice — installs the expiration handler, withdraws a pending request on a session end or a hard stop, and completes the delivered task **exactly once**: `completeHeldTask()` is the probe's `completeBackgroundTask(success:)` idiom with the duplicate guard supplied by `MeshContinuationDriver.consumePendingCompletion()` answering nil on a second read, and the handle is dropped before it is completed. It **keeps the tunnel** — the probe tears its own down and the product must not — so there is no radio verb, no listener, no routed access gate and no teardown anywhere in the file; the one way it reaches a radio is `FernletStore.setMeshContinuation(state:lastAudit:)`, which assigns the projection and re-runs `ProximityRunPolicy` (the store's sixth own edge). It is deliberately never called from inside `runProximityPolicy`, because that setter re-runs the funnel and a re-entrant pass would leave the outer one diffing against a `previous` the inner one had replaced. **Nothing spins**: no `Timer`, no `DispatchQueue`, no stored `Task` handle and no `unowned` host (ML1/ML4 do not apply); the progress bar rides `ProximitySessionPoller`'s existing 30-second tick, reading `MeshNetworkManager.sessionContinuationReading` and ratcheting through `MeshContinuationProgress`. Both bounds are R2 literals — eight submissions per session (item 4's `running + appForegrounded → requested` row re-arms on every foreground return) and eight registered identifiers per process (`BGTaskScheduler.register` treats a duplicate as a programmer error). An ownerless delivery is ended at once and presents nothing; a second delivery is completed `false` and dropped. The card's two sentences are rendered from `MeshContinuationCopy`'s `LocalizedStringResource`s with `String(localized:)` at the submit site — the app target IS `Bundle.main`, so no `bundle:` argument applies. No persisted surface, so the wipe wall is owed nothing. Walled by `MeshContinuationTaskHostTests` (the five acts over two fakes) and `MeshContinuationTaskHostWallTests` (the framework's two homes, the forbidden-needle list, the feed's single call site, the funnel's silence, the mesh's Live-Activity suppression, the retired 1:1 anchors (no proximity Live Activity spelled anywhere in ProximityKit or `FernletSocial`, and, since plan step A0.6.4, the proximity attributes type named in the app only in its reaper's own file, `ProximityLiveActivityReaper.swift`) and the plist wildcard). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MeshContinuationScheduling.swift` | **P8 item 6**, the `BackgroundTasks` SEAM: `BackgroundContinuationScheduling` (register / submit / cancel), `ContinuationTaskHandle` (progress, copy, expiration handler, complete) and `ContinuationTaskRequest`, plus the two production conformers `SystemContinuationScheduler` and `SystemContinuationTaskHandle`. The seam exists because `BGTaskScheduler` refuses a continued-processing submission on a Simulator outright (error 1) and nothing can make iOS deliver a task to a unit test: without it, item 6's five acts would have zero tier-1 coverage. Outside the `#if DEBUG` feasibility probe this is the ONLY app file that names `BGTaskScheduler` or `BGContinuedProcessingTask`, which `MeshContinuationTaskHostWallTests` pins. What it copies from the probe is named line by line in its header — the pre-register and pre-submit `cancel`, `register(forTaskWithIdentifier:using: nil)` with the wrong-class arm completing `false` and a `Task { @MainActor }` hop, `BGContinuedProcessingTaskRequest(identifier:title:subtitle:)` with `strategy = .fail`, the progress scale, the `expirationHandler` hop and `updateTitle(_:subtitle:)`. What it does NOT copy is `endProbe`'s `stopNetworkOperations()`: §25.1's production shutdown keeps the tunnel. `requiredResources` is left at its default because the probe never set it either, and the default is what ran on hardware (§15.1, 2026-09-02). The conformers are the untested remainder and are item 9's device rows. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RoutedShareRefusalCopy.swift` | The P5 review's finding 5: `RoutedShareRefusalCopy`, the app-side fork of `MeshRoutedShareRefusal` into `LocalizedStringKey` copy — one sentence per frozen cause, an exhaustive `switch` so a new cause is a build error until it has a sentence, and the session alert's existing "Session" title. Presented by `DisposableCameraView`'s session alert, which shows the typed refusal ahead of the legacy `meshError` `String`. The wording is the owner's; the shape is not. `chatNotice(.filtered)` (2026-10-15) is the send filter's calm in-place line. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RoutedDeliveryHoldBanner.swift` | P5 item 9: `RoutedDeliveryHoldCopy` (the testable copy enum — `LocalizedStringKey` only, one sentence pair per cause, no count interpolated into any key, frozen a11y id `friends.deliveryHold`), `RoutedDeliveryHoldDismissal` (the dismissal keyed to the FACT — same cause, count not grown — so one tap cannot silence a later, different hold for the life of the process) and `RoutedDeliveryHoldBanner`, the Friends-tab banner mounted from `ConnectView` and gated on `MeshNetworkManager.routedDeliveryHold`. Dismissal is `@State`, so nothing is persisted and nothing is owed the wipe wall; the card carries no `.accessibilityElement(children: .combine)`, because combining would strip the dismiss `Button`'s trait, focus and label. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ConnectView.swift` | Friends tab photo wall, nearby-discovery status, connection-success transition, the keep-as-friends prompt, and full-screen saved-photo feed. Presents `DisposableCameraView` while a Friends session is active. The album pushes Activities, Friends & Blocks and the friend shop as `FriendsRoute` path values, so a re-tap of the Friends tab pops them; the path is cleared when a session that swapped the album for the camera ends, and the friend shop is popped when its post-session window lapses or closes. The session transition is split in two (P6 item 2): `handleSessionSurfaceChange` on `isInSession` owns the layout swap and camera chrome, `handleCommittedPeerChange` on `hasCommittedPeer` owns the choreography and triggers the review — which presents only once `isSessionLive` is false. Its heal arm dismisses the standing prompt and clears `showConnectionAnimation`, because a cover that never presented latches `.accessibilityHidden(true)` on the whole surface. **Since session photos U3 (2026-09-30) the session-end PHOTO review is not this view's**: `SessionPhotoReviewCoordinator` presents it app-wide in its own overlay window. This view keeps the candidates-only keep-friends prompt (`presentDisconnectReviewIfNeeded`, asked with `hasPhotos: false` only), which never presents while photos are outstanding, the overlay shows or the review blocks discovery, and a prompt already up is withdrawn unconsumed on the overlay's rising edge (`handleOverlayChange`, batch cleared first — I17); every trigger schedules a short-deferred check that never requests over the camera's own sheets or the root sheet, and a landing watchdog withdraws an unlanded request, unconsumed, and re-asks a bounded number of times. It renders `PendingPhotoReviewCard` ("Photos waiting for you" → `reopen()`) in the album's nearby-status slot while photos wait — never over a live session or under a duress decoy — and withholds the launch restore's resume OFFER while the review blocks discovery (`reviewCoordinator.resumePresentation(_:)`). Since 2026-10-15 the feed's post carries a flag on another person's photo (App Review guideline 1.2): the feed hosts `peerReportFlow`, and filing it reports the sender and deletes the photo (`reportWallPhoto`). |

`SessionPhotoReviewHost` (the four store facts it needs, held weakly); and `SessionPhotoReviewCoordinator` (`@MainActor @Observable`, owned by `FernletStore`): snapshots the batch at present (all ticked, toggle off, candidates by eligibility), leaves an ended held mesh AT PRESENT (door 3; `leaveEndedMeshIfHeld`), answers through `finishReviewedPhotos` + `completeFriendReview` (candidates once, I17), exports to Photos only after the answer over `keptOnWall` from the wall through the store-injected saver (I2), stays up with the inline failure on `notApplied` (I25), waits for the leave ("Ending the session...") before any hide, re-presents for late arrivals, hides without answering on duress / delete-all / First Aid (at once, not after the settle), and exposes `blocksDiscovery` = not live AND (photos outstanding OR an answer OR a leave in flight) for the run policy (I11, I21) and `resumePresentation(_:)` for the Friends tab. **Fix round 1 (U3-C-U3-R1):** no answer is ever stranded — its `saveFailureAcknowledgement` (the overlay's) is opened at present and closed by both hides, `developSaveFailureAcknowledgement` (the camera's, held here rather than in the camera's `@State`) follows `cameraDevelopReviewUp`, so a failed export's wait ends with its review; the gate waits for `answerInFlight`; and only the overlay's own answer (`ownAnswerInFlight`) refuses its Keep / Delete all / Not now. `SessionPhotoReviewTriggers` is ContentView's modifier: the window-scene reader, the fed facts, an evaluation on every manager/store edge. Two stored tasks (settle, leave) cancelled in `isolated deinit`. ## Wellbeing, Stress, And First Aid

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FirstAidView.swift` | `FirstAidView` / `FirstAidTool` — calm first-aid sheet routing to breathing, 5-4-3-2-1 grounding, and the Worry Box, plus the static region-keyed crisis-line card (`CrisisSupportCard`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CrisisSupportCard.swift` | `CrisisSupportCard` — the gentle-support card: the crisis line for the device's region (`CrisisResources`) with its call/text buttons, or supportive copy and no number where Fernlet has no verified line. Extracted from First Aid's support row (2026-10-16) so Settings → Safety & reporting carries the same card; each host names its accessibility-identifier prefix. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BreathingExerciseView.swift` | `BreathingExerciseView` — animated breathing circle (box 4-4-4-4 / relax 4-7-8, 1–3 min, optional haptics); a completed session reports its interval to the caller for Apple Health. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GroundingView.swift` | `GroundingView` — 5-4-3-2-1 grounding: a stepped tap-to-advance sensory flow with a per-sense colour wash, ending on a soft affirmation. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorryBoxView.swift` | Worry Box surfaces: `WorryEntryView` write-and-let-go entry flow (hosted in First Aid) and the Private-hub kept-worries list with per-worry release. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorryBoxService.swift` | `WorryBoxService` — app-side owner of sealed, local-only Worry Box notes (hub-key read while the Private tab is open by passcode or tap, device-key write fallback while closed, folded on open; never synced, absent from SealedBackup). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PrivateHubOpenCoordinator.swift` | `PrivateHubOpenCoordinator` (`FernletPrivateHubOpening`) — the no-passcode Private tab's open: tap opens the device-custody key, and before any fresh key it checks for entries no key here can open (card first, removal only on the user's tap, latches and every v2 restore marker / accepted / observed head cleared; a hidden kind is counted only as "other private entries", and the card promises a Sealed backup restore only when one will really run — since unit B3 every restore is a merge, so openable journal rows that stay behind no longer withhold it); `PrivateHubKeyCustody` / `PriorPrivateEntryStore` seams and the production `SealedPriorEntryStore` (its device keys read without minting through the shared `SealedDeviceKeyRead`; its `bookkeepingCleared` hook lets the app drop every pending Sealed backup choice when the bookkeeping goes). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SealedBackupRestoreHold.swift` | `SealedBackupRestoreHold` — the persisted `fernlet.sealedBackup.restoreAwaitsOwner` bit the app-lock reset funnel sets, plus `fernlet.sealedBackup.preResetCopies` (the payloads whose backup was on at the reset); while set every AMBIENT sealed-backup restore skips and every re-upload (and the escrow adopt's re-seal) of a recorded payload is held, so the pre-reset cloud copy stays as it was. A payload whose chunk set is deleted since leaves the record and uploads again (kept by delete-all). `release()` — Privacy & Data's device-owner-checked "Restore" — drops only the ambient-restore bit: each payload stays recorded (its uploads held) until its own restore lands (`.restored` / `.nothingToRestore`), so a pre-reset copy is pulled back before anything replaces it. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/StressService.swift` | `StressService` (`StressScoringContextProviding`) — opt-in body-signals stress estimate joining HealthKit HRV/RHR/respiration/temp with diary confounders via `StressEngine`; baselines in a device-local sidecar. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletScoring/StressEngine.swift` | Pure personal-baseline stress estimator (`StressDaySample`) mapping HRV/resting-HR deviation to gentle wellness states. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/StressExplainerSheet.swift` | `StressExplainerSheet` — gentle "how we estimate this" body-signals explainer (wellness framing, not-medical disclaimer) that can chain into First Aid. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GentleOffers.swift` | `GentleOfferKind` + pure gating/rotation logic for the once-per-day ambient gentle-offer card (breathing / worry box / short walk). |

## Workouts And Guided Training

| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutSetupView.swift` | `WorkoutSetupSheet` — captures durable workout context: split, training location/equipment, weekly frequency, experience, session length and stretch switches (`WorkoutSessionSection`), interests, areas to work around (`WorkoutInjurySection`), and the way into the user's own exercises, hidden exercises and saved swaps (`WorkoutExercisePreferencesEntry`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutSessionSection.swift` | `WorkoutSessionSection` — the setup sheet's session fields: session length (No limit · 20 · 30 · 45 · 60 min), warm-up stretches (Full · Short · Off) and the cool-down switch, saved as `WorkoutSessionOptions`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutExercisePreferencesView.swift` | `WorkoutExercisePreferencesEntry` (Workout setup's "Your exercises" row) and `WorkoutExercisePreferencesSheet` — the user's own exercises (edit, delete with a confirm, "Add an exercise"), the exercises hidden from suggestions ("Show again") and the saved swaps ("Remove"), each saved as it happens. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CustomExerciseFormSheet.swift` | `CustomExerciseFormSheet` — the hand-made custom exercise form: name, main and other muscles, movement and direction, kind, equipment and alternatives, required gear, reps or seconds, one side at a time, difficulty (`CustomExerciseDraft`), with the name and cap checks. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ExerciseSwapSheet.swift` | `ExerciseSwapSheet` — the plan editor's Swap: up to four stand-ins the planner would allow here (`WorkoutExerciseSwapOptions`), then a search over every exercise. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore+ExercisePreferences.swift` | `FernletStore` exercise preferences and custom exercises: hide / show again, save / remove an alternate, the editor's pending marks in one save, swap options, and add / edit / delete a custom exercise (mirrored into `WorkoutExerciseCatalog`; logs untouched). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutInjuryPicker.swift` | `WorkoutInjurySection` — the setup sheet's "Areas to work around": a None · Mild · Severe menu per body area, the rested-muscle chips, the free-text note and a line naming what the note leaves out. `WorkoutInjuryDraft` — its open (older chips read back as severe areas; the note's areas left to the note) and save rules (severe mirrored into the avoided sets older builds read, mild never). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutLocationSetupView.swift` | `WorkoutLocationSetupView` — location creator + equipment picker: choose where you train, then check off equipment in a categorized grid mapped to coarse planning capabilities, with "Weights I have" below it for a free-weight space. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutWeightInventorySection.swift` | `WorkoutWeightInventorySection` — the location editor's optional "Weights I have": dumbbell presets (home set, adjustable, full rack) and pairs, kettlebell sizes, a bar with plate-pair steppers and the loads they make, custom weights, in the user's unit (`WorkoutWeightInventory`); `WeightInventoryText` formats them like the runner. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutPlanningService.swift` | `WorkoutPlanningService` — split recommendation, day-plan generation (with the load context, the 48-hour guard's recent training and the exercise preferences), and AI day-plan adjustment extracted from `FernletStore` (owns the FoundationModels workout dependency). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutRecentTrainingBuilder.swift` | `WorkoutRecentTrainingBuilder` — what the 48-hour guard reads from the day history: the primary muscles yesterday's hard working sets reached (guided `ExerciseResult`s and parsed lines; never ramps, holds, stretches or today's rows) and yesterday's workout names. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutMeasurementEntry.swift` | `WorkoutMeasurementEntry` — workout distance and treadmill speed at the entry/display boundary (2026-10-20, idea 16): stored in miles and mph as always, typed and read in kilometres and km/h on the `.metric` system and in miles and mph on `.us` and `.uk` (since 2026-10-20, N9: the UK reads road distance in miles, as Apple Fitness does; loads keep the `.us`-only lb/kg rule), and `drawnLine(_:)` for a logged or planned line (planner descriptors localized, mph shown in km/h). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutLoadContextBuilder.swift` | `WorkoutLoadContextBuilder` — builds the planner's `WorkoutLoadContext` from the day history: structured `ExerciseResult`s (guided, entered set by set, or corrected later) and parsed hand-logged lines per lift (a lift a hand-logged row's own record covers is read from the record; guided rows' other lines skipped — they record the plan, not the lift), two weeks of sleep and protein, the last calibration, the locale's unit. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedRunLoadLog.swift` | `GuidedRunLoadLog` — the plan⇄run-state crossings for load tracking: `LoadGuidance` → run `Tracking` at start (suggestion preselected), run `Tracking` → `ExerciseResult`s and the rebuilt logged text at finish (skipped lifts logged as skipped, never as results); and each counted lift's last-time numbers from the history rollup (`lastTimes(from:)`), baked into the run for the Live Activity. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedWorkoutLoadViews.swift` | The guided runner's load panel: the −/+ weight picker with the prescribed range, calibration cues, the once-in-a-while override question, the optional Easy · Good · Tough tap, Edit reps (and "Edit reps for <previous lift>"), plus the store seam that edits the live run. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutSetEditing.swift` | Correcting sets after the fact and the Log sheet's set-by-set entry (idea 6): `SetRowDraft` (reps, the weight as typed, read locale-tolerantly), `ExerciseSetsDraft` (one lift's rows, unit, effort and override reason, from a logged result, a hand-written line or a new entry), and `WorkoutSetEditing` — which lifts of a workout are editable (records, and a hand-logged row's sets × reps lines; never a Health import) and how an edit writes back (the record re-recorded and its line rewritten together). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutSetEditorViews.swift` | The shared set editor: `ExerciseSetsEditor` (rows, Add set, Repeat last set, lb/kg, Easy · Good · Tough, the override reason), `SetRowEditor` (reps stepper, weight field, remove — each named for VoiceOver and Voice Control, stacked at accessibility sizes), `SetEffortChips`, `SetOverrideReasonChips`, and `SetLoadFormat`'s system unit words. Used by the Edit sheet and the Log sheet. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedWarmUpSets.swift` | Warm-up sets and the plate calculator in the runner (idea 12): `GuidedWarmUpWords` ("Warm-up set 1 of 3", VoiceOver's and spoken cues' lines, the line under the card), `GuidedPlanRow` / `GuidedWarmUpPlanRow` (a lift's warm-ups folded into one plan row), `WarmUpSetsToggle` and `FernletStore.setWorkoutWarmUpSets(_:)` (the synced "Warm-up sets before heavy lifts" switch), `GuidedPlateText` and `GuidedPlatesDisclosure` ("Plates": each side's plates on a standard bar in the lift's unit, or "Closest: 135 lb"; spoken in words). |

| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedZones.swift` | Heart-rate zones for guided cardio (idea 9): `WorkoutZonePreferences` (device-local "Heart rate during cardio" / "Zone nudges" switches, both off, KEPT by Delete everything), `GuidedZonePlan` (when a live session is wanted; the cue a run shows — the talk test, or the reading against its target; the 20 s freshness rule), `GuidedZoneNudgeTracker` (30 s drift after a 30 s settle, once per drift, at most once a minute), `GuidedZoneDirector` (owned by `FernletStore`: starts and ends the live session with the run, keeps the reading in memory only, plays the nudge, re-publishes the Live Activity at most every 5 s) and `GuidedZoneWords` (the runner's talk-test lines). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedZoneViews.swift` | `GuidedZoneReadout` — the runner card's live "118 bpm · aim 110–125" with its calm word ("in range" / "a little high" / "a little low"), one VoiceOver element; `WorkoutZoneSettingsRows` — the two heart-rate switches in Workout setup's "Cues during a workout". |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/LiveHeartRateSession.swift` | `LiveHeartRateSource` — the seam the zone director drives, with the gateway's `HealthKitLiveHeartRateSource` as its production conformer; the header records which devices deliver live heart rate to an iPhone-only session (BLE GATT straps, Powerbeats Pro 2, AirPods Pro 3 — not Apple Watch in real time) and why the session's workout is discarded. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PlannedWorkoutRun.swift` | Planned and coach-imported workouts in the guided runner (idea 17): `PlannedWorkoutRun` (reads a plan's lines with `ExerciseLineParser`, builds the session as written with the warm-up seam left empty, and the workout a finished run logs), `PlannedRunLink` (the plan's id, day and lines as written, between the row's Start and the runner's) and the store seam (`guidedSession(forPlanned:date:)`, `logPlannedRun`, which completes the planned row as "log it" does). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedRunCues.swift` | `GuidedRunCuePlan` — the runner's cue rules as pure functions of the run state (which move cues what, what the run's clock is due to cue, what the background carries); `GuidedRunCueDirector` — owned by `FernletStore`, re-armed on every run change, plays rest-end / hold-start / hold-end / set / block / finish cues while the app is in front and keeps one rest-end or hold-end local notification pending for a phone in the background (never prompting for permission); also says each moment through `FernletSpeaker` when Spoken cues is on, in front only, keeping off VoiceOver while the runner sheet (`runnerOnScreen`) already announces it. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedRunSpokenCues.swift` | `GuidedRunSpokenCue` — the words of the runner's opt-in spoken cues as pure functions of the run state (idea 11): the next exercise and side, a new lift's suggested weight and last time (`WorkoutActivityLoadText`'s spoken clauses, no range), an interval's pace and round, "Rest's over.", "Hold now." / "Go — hard.", "Hold's done.", "Time's up on this part.", "Workout done."; plus the weight clause VoiceOver's own move line gains. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutCuePreferences.swift` | `WorkoutCuePreferences` — the device-local Workout haptics (default on) / Workout sounds (default off) / Spoken cues (default off) switches as frozen UserDefaults keys, KEPT by Delete everything; `WorkoutCueSettingsSection` — their field in the Workout setup sheet. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/fernlet-cue-chime.wav`, `fernlet-cue-tick.wav`, `fernlet-cue-silent.wav` | The workout cue sounds (quiet sine tones written for Fernlet, 22.05 kHz mono PCM), named by `FernletSound` and by the background cue notifications; the silent file lets a haptics-only background cue buzz without a tone. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedWorkout.swift` | `GuidedWorkoutSheet` — in-app guided workout runner (current exercise, set X of Y, live rest countdown with "−15 s" / "+15 s" chips) driven by `store.guidedRunState` and mirrored to the Live Activity. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedWorkoutEditorSheet.swift` | `GuidedWorkoutEditorSheet` — manual editor for a suggested session (per-exercise sets/reps/rest override, remove, reorder, add from catalog, Swap with "Always use this instead", "Don't suggest" — `GuidedEditorPreferences`); saving replaces the session in today's plan and writes the pending preferences. |

Never read on the AI path (Gym Visual licence; `ExerciseDemonstrationTests`). |
