| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ActivityModels.swift` | Pure value types for proximity group activities: host-signed versioned rosters, join tokens, and `ActivityLimits`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ProximityCoordinatorEnums.swift` | `ProximityMode` (trainer or friend), the session mode Fernlet persists on its trust and audit records, with the frozen raw values of ProximityKit's own `ProximitySessionMode`, which `FernletConnections` converts from case for case (`ProximityMode+SessionMode.swift`). The role, ranging mode and session mode a coordinator reports are ProximityKit's (`Engine/ProximitySessionEnums.swift`). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ProximityPersistenceRecords.swift` | Codable proximity trust/audit DTOs (`ProximityTrustedPeerRecord`, and `TrainerAuditEvent`, the row `FernletConnections` converts ProximityKit's session audits into) for the persisted, synced trust vault. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FDADailyValues.swift` | The single FDA Daily Value reference table shared by the gap analyzer and the label scanner, verified against 21 CFR 101.9. Flat label-comparison reference — no age/sex adjustment, no upper intake levels. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/GroceryAggregation.swift` | Pure, catalog-free shopping-list aggregation (F3, §11.3): merges several recipes' ingredients into one consolidated list. Below the wall, no I/O — `FoodItem` resolution stays the caller's job. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeScaling.swift` | Pure proportional recipe scaling (F4, §11.4). A non-persisted view/share-time transform returning scaled *copies* — it never mutates a stored recipe, resolves no `FoodItem`, and touches no catalog. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeSubstitution.swift` | A bound substitution suggestion: a catalog `FoodItem` proposed to replace one ingredient, plus an optional display-only reason. The model picks a candidate *number*; code binds it back to the catalog. A fork of a multipart recipe keeps its parts, the substitute re-pointed into the replaced row's part. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeComponents.swift` | Multipart recipes (2026-09-24): `RecipeComponent`, an id PARTITION over a recipe's flat `ingredients`/`steps` (never a copy, so nutrition, scaling, grocery and old readers count every row once), and `RecipeDefinition.resolvedComponents`, the one resolution rule (unclaimed rows join the last part, empty parts drop, fewer than two collapse to one, at most 12). Also `RecipeComponentNaming`, the editor's `RecipeComponentInput` + `RecipeComponentAssembly` (parts → rows + partition through the one-part upsert/sanitizer), and `cookingSteps` (making order with part names). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeComponentWire.swift` | The multipart half of the `fernlet.recipe` wire: version stays 1, the flat arrays carry the whole recipe with section-labelled steps (`RecipeComponentWire`, label token ": "), and the optional `components` partition (`SharedRecipeComponent`, strict decode, `isValidPartition`) lets a newer reader rebuild the parts (`componentSlices`, `RecipeComponentImport.layout`) while an older one reads a flat recipe. `assembled`, `droppingComponents` (the old-reader form) and `withoutSteps` (the notes toggle) live here. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/HeartDropTransport.swift` | The `HeartDropRecord` (rotating pairwise day tag + sealed blob) and the `HeartDropTransporting` seam. The transport neither knows nor can learn who either endpoint is — this is why the wall holds across CloudKit. |

## Food, Nutrition, And Recipe Services

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/FoodDataCatalog.swift` | USDA food item record decoder (compact + raw FDC), source-JSON loading for the DB generator, `FoodItemSearch` scorer, and recipe search helpers. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/FoodCatalog.swift` | `FoodCatalog` service: merges the SQLite bundled food source with the in-memory user-item snapshot; search via FTS candidate prefilter → `FoodItemSearch` scorer, plus ID/exact-name resolution. Replaces the old in-memory `allFoodItems` array. Also holds the fix-1.10 correction-alias snapshot (`setSearchAliases`), which promotes the user's own remembered pick to rank 1 on `results(for:)` — never on the bind-confidence `scoredResults`. Since F9b also the recipe-pick snapshot (`setRecipeSearchPicks`), answered only by `.ingredientIdentity` searches; on that surface `candidates(for:limit:ranking:)` puts the remembered answer for the WHOLE description first, exempt from the dish demotion (F9b fix round 1 — the sub-phrases never spell "2% milk" or a fourth word), while the resolver's `.standard` pool keeps the per-sub-phrase promotion only. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/BundledFoodStore.swift` | `BundledFoodSource` protocol + read-only SQLite-backed `SQLiteBundledFoodSource` (FTS5 candidate query, id/exact lookups, hydration) and `InMemoryBundledFoodSource` for tests; shared `FoodCatalogSchema`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/BundledRowCorrection.swift` | Load-time corrections every hydrated SQLite row passes through, fixing defects in the committed catalog without regenerating it: the branded per-100 g basis rebase (F2, a 100 g serving plus the label serving as an `each` portion) and the raw FDC serving-unit aliases (F1(a), `GRM`/`GM` → `g`, `MLT` → `ml`), and the retype of packaged products filed as SR Legacy (F6: by category, plus ten filed under an SR food group, by frozen FDC id). Pure, per-row, O(1). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/BundledFoodSupplement.swift` | `BundledFoodSupplement` (reads `Resources/FoodCatalogSupplement.json`, the 26 zero-energy SR Legacy foods the catalog build dropped) and `SupplementedBundledFoodSource`, which serves the base SQLite catalog plus those rows as one bundled source (FTS-equivalent prefix-AND gate, id/batch/exact lookups). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/TypeaheadDuplicateCollapse.swift` | One row per catalog name (within a data type) in a typed search: keeps the physically plausible row whose tap default converts, at the group's first position; never touches user items or logged rows. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/CuratedSearchAlias.swift` | The curated typed-search alias table (ingredient-search round F7): frozen English phrases ("chocolate chip(s)", "garlic clove(s)", "red pepper flakes", "vegetable oil") naming the USDA row whose name does not say them, by catalog id; prefix-aware on the last word, "semi sweet" folded to "semisweet". Typed search only — inserted beneath a correction and this person's own/logged rows, never reaching the resolver pool, the importer's bind or a bind score. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/Resources/FoodCatalogSupplement.json` | The 26 zero-energy SR Legacy foods (salt, baking soda, waters, teas) served beside `FoodCatalog.sqlite`, in the compact source schema. Generated — edit only via `Scripts/food-catalog/sr_zero_energy_supplement.py`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodCatalogDatabaseBuilder.swift` | Build-time generator that converts the `FoodDataSource/*.json` foods into the shipped read-only `FoodCatalog.sqlite` (run via the gated `FoodCatalogGenerationTests`). Not used at runtime. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/food-catalog/` | Offline, standard-library preservation pipeline for the approved FDC/FNDDS/curated-branded/CoFID source set; builds only ignored validation artifacts and never replaces the shipping catalog. Its README is the reproducibility and corrective-workflow contract. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/food-catalog/sr_zero_energy_supplement.py` | Not part of that pipeline: regenerates `FoodCatalog/Resources/FoodCatalogSupplement.json` by rule from USDA's SR Legacy 2018-04 JSON (manifest-verified size and SHA-256) — the zero-energy SR foods the committed catalog's build dropped — after proving its field mapping reproduces all 7,761 SR rows the catalog kept. |
| `Fernlet/Scripts/food-catalog/misfiled_branded_audit.py` | Not part of that pipeline either: proves FoodCatalog's load-time retype of packaged products filed as SR Legacy (`BundledRowCorrection`, both rules read out of the Swift source) picks exactly the rows FDC's own `food.csv` types `branded_food` — 773 = 763 by category + 10 by FDC id — and leaves only `sr_legacy_food`/`foundation_food` rows `srLegacy`. Reads the manifest-pinned 2026-04-30 FDC CSV archive (size, SHA-256 and the `food.csv` member hash checked); exits non-zero on any disagreement. |
| `Fernlet/FoodDataSource/FoodCatalogSourceManifest.json` | Immutable source/version/hash/license/attribution contract, strict FDC allowlist, and explicit CNF/NEVO exclusions for the offline preservation pipeline. |
| `Fernlet/FoodDataSource/FoodCatalogGenerationEvidence.json` | Compact measured validation, FTS, preservation, size/ODR arithmetic, and committed-catalog checksum evidence for the historical schema-v3 local generation; current schema-v4 reproducibility results are recorded in the round ledger. Contains no raw data or SQLite payload. |
| `Fernlet/Docs/Food-Catalog-Runtime-ODR-Options-2026-08-24.md` | Measured base/ODR/preservation distribution options, P0/P1/P2 projection boundary, and the owner decision required before any runtime catalog or ODR integration. |
| `Fernlet/FoodDataSource/USDAFoodItems.json` | Source USDA food item dataset consumed by the build-time DB generator (not bundled at runtime). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/Resources/FoodCatalog.sqlite` | The shipped read-only SQLite food catalog (FTS5), generated from `FoodDataSource/` and loaded via `Bundle.module`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutExercises.json` | Bundled exercise dataset consumed by the workout system: muscles, tool (plus alternatives and required gear), movement pattern and plane, modality, difficulty (1-3) and measure (reps or seconds) per row. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/FoundationFoodSelection.swift` | Foundation model availability and food selection model helpers. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/NutritionLabelScanner.swift` | Nutrition label scan result model, scan errors, and scanner implementation. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/NutritionLabelCameraSheet.swift` | Camera and image picker UI for capturing nutrition labels and reviewing scan results. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/RecipeWebImporter.swift` | Recipe import pipeline for extracting structured recipes from web content and metadata. With no nutrition label it estimates macros from the ingredient lines against the USDA catalog: units read longest first and must end where they end, count/size words bind "each" only on a row with a count portion, and a line that cannot be counted is skipped and counted (`ImportedRecipe.uncountedIngredientCount`) rather than voiding the page (ingredient-search round F11); Unicode fractions ("½", "1½") fold to ASCII first, a bare count ("1 lemon") counts one serving only where the bound row's serving is one item (never SR's 100 g reference amount), and a line that opens with an amount but cannot be read past it counts as left out. F4b: a line the bound row's own data cannot weigh is weighed by the USDA typical size of the ingredient that row IS (`TypicalPortionTable`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeShareCodec.swift` | The `fernlet.recipe` v1 payload: builds it for the proximity mesh (`proximityPayload`, the `.local` arm carrying a multipart recipe's `components` partition; older builds read the flattened recipe), and reads pasted text (`decodePayload`). Since 2026-09-30 it no longer WRITES the paste text — the share sheet sends `RecipeShareText` — but still reads the text older builds shared (the frozen `legacyPayloadMarker`, "Fernlet recipe data:") and the bare JSON the Shortcuts file import hands it. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeShareText.swift` | `RecipeShareText` (2026-09-30) — the readable text "Share as text" hands the system share sheet: name, servings, one per-serving macro line (calories only when shown), ingredients (under each part's name, with the recipe page's amounts), numbered steps, notes and a web recipe's lines, estimate note and source. No JSON and no Fernlet data (the owner's report). Labels localize in the sender's language; recipe content stays verbatim. "Include notes" off withholds the notes and — for a recipe the person made — the steps, as the nearby share does. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeShareTextItemSource.swift` | `RecipeShareTextItemSource` (2026-09-30) — the `UIActivityItemSource` "Share as text" hands `ActivityShareView`: every activity gets the readable string, and the recipe's name is the sheet's title (a local, title-only `LPLinkMetadata` — no URL, no fetch) and Mail's subject. Chosen over `ShareLink`, whose string form leaves the title blank and whose `SharePreview` form drops Copy for Save to Files (measured on the iOS 26.5 simulator). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeMessageComposer.swift` | `RecipeMessageComposer` + `RecipeMessagesSendOutcome` (2026-09-30) — the `MFMessageComposeViewController` behind "Send in Messages", holding only the Fernlet recipe card (`FernletMessagesCard`); the delegate hops to the main actor. `canSendText` is read when the row is drawn and again at the tap, because presenting a composer where Messages is not set up (every simulator) throws. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealBuilder.swift` | Converts a `FoodSelectionPlan` and food candidates into structured `Meal` records (one serving of a matching saved recipe, else the matched foods), with good-protein threshold logic. A multi-ingredient item's recipe is OFFERED for the pre-log review (`PlanResult.offeredRecipes`), never minted, and never backs another item (since 2026-10-16). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealReviewRecipeOffer.swift` | One recipe the pre-log review sheet offers (the decomposition tier's suggested recipe or a plan tier's offered one) with the person's choices — keep it or not, name, yield — and the rebuild of the recipe to mint from the reviewed component grams. The sheet mints only the offers still switched on (bug confirmation 2026-10-15, food-ai §4). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/CustomIngredientUpsert.swift` | Resolves manual recipe ingredient inputs into `FoodItem` records, creating or updating custom food entries in the catalog. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/PreciseMacros.swift` | `PreciseMacros` — decimal ingredient grams ("3.4 g protein", 2026-09-29), the side channel beside whole-gram `Macros`: a sanitizing init, `rounded` by the same rule as `Macros.scaled(by:)`, and `scaled(by:)` mirroring it exactly, so whole-gram foods keep every number. `roundedToTenths` / `scaledToTenths(by:)` are the tenth-of-a-gram precision a decimal food is stored, scaled and shown at, so a row's "2.5 g" always counts 3 g. Stored on `FoodItem.preciseMacros` and the `fernlet.recipe` wire's optional `preciseMacros` key; never in the hash-covered exchange packet. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/MacroGramEntry.swift` | `MacroGramEntry` — the typed-grams rule for macro fields: parses either decimal separator through `LocaleTolerantNumber`, rejects negatives and garbage, stores tenths of a gram, and displays in the locale's separator with no grouping and no ".0", so a prefill always parses back. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/RecipeQuantityDisplay.swift` | `RecipeQuantityDisplay` — an ingredient quantity or serving size for a row that also shows `MacroGramEntry` grams (the recipe editor's collapsed ingredient row, the catalog suggestion row): up to two decimal places in the locale's separator, never grouped, so "1,5 cup · P3,4g" never mixes separators the way `%g` did. Display only. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealResolutionService.swift` | `MealResolutionService` + `MealPlausibility` — the quick-log meal-resolution cascade (cold whole-description probe → AI decomposition → AI selection → lexicon → plan → heuristic) plus the micronutrient fallback and implausible-total gate. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WholeDescriptionFoodProbe.swift` | Cold pre-decomposition whole-description resolver probe: requires the corpus-calibrated high score floor plus a typed-unit `FoodPortion`, computes its grams, emits only a validated food-serving basis, and conservatively surfaces stripped brand/retailer text. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoundationDishDecomposition.swift` | `FoundationDishDecompositionModel` — on-device Foundation Models decomposition of a meal description into catalog-resolved, scaled `Meal` components with a match confidence. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DishTemplateLexicon.swift` | `DishTemplateLexicon` + JSON template types — loads `DishTemplates.json` for deterministic, count-aware dish-template lookup (the decomposition fallback path). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodCaptureRouter.swift` | `FoodCaptureRoute` + router — auto-detects a captured photo (barcode / nutrition label / meal) and routes to the matching existing flow, or `.ambiguous` for a gentle chooser. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealPhotoRecognizer.swift` | `MealPhotoRecognizer` (+ host protocol) — on-device Vision food-photo classification that composes a text description into the existing `resolveMeals` cascade, always pausing at review. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/MealPhotoPolaroid.swift` | `MealPhotoPolaroid` — polaroid-framed meal-photo tile with lazy sealed-byte load and `MealPhotoPresence` classification (here / on other device / unreadable). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PhotoSaveFailureAcknowledgement.swift` | `PhotoSaveFailureAcknowledgement` — the session-photo review hosts' one-shot wait for a failed "Also save kept photos to Photos" alert to be closed before the review leaves the session and hides (design §4.6's order: commit, export, the alert inside the review, then the leave and the hide). Resumed when the failure binding clears or the sheet disappears, so a torn-down sheet never strands the answer. Since U3 its two hosts are `SessionPhotoReviewCoordinator` (the overlay, resumed by `SessionPhotoReviewScreen`) and the camera's Develop sheet. **U3 fix round 1 (U3-C-U3-R1):** it waits only while the review that shows the alert is OPEN — `reviewDidOpen()` / `reviewDidClose()` (a close also resumes a waiter), starting closed; a wait begun after the review went returns at once and audits `sessionPhotoReview.exportFailureUnseen` (context `host`), because that wait never ended and held the discovery block's answer leg for the process. Both instances live on the coordinator (`host` "overlay" / "develop"). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecentBites.swift` | `RecentBite` + `MealPhotoPresence` — pure 7-day windowing behind Home's "Recent bites" photo strip (resolves which photographed meals to show without reading sealed bytes). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BarcodeScanView.swift` | `BarcodeResolveFlowView` / `BarcodeScanView` — scan a product barcode and resolve it (user items → catalog → gentle not-found that creates a food item with the barcode remembered). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BrandedCatalogResourceLoader.swift` | `BrandedCatalogResourceLoader` — loads the ~364k-product branded food DB (`FoodCatalogBranded.sqlite`) via bundle-first or On-Demand Resource and attaches it as a secondary `FoodCatalog` source; refuses a zero-row file (the placeholder pack a build without the gitignored DB ships). The `branded-food-catalog` tag lives on the `ODRAssets/` synchronized folder in the project (see `ODRAssets/README.md`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodProductWebImporter.swift` | `FoodProductWebImporter` / `FoodProductWebSearch` / `ImportedFoodProduct` — on-device Foundation Models import of a branded product's nutrition from a web product page. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/OpenFoodFactsClient.swift` | `OpenFoodFactsClient` — the optional online UPC lookup's ONE network seam: a read-only `GET` to `world.openfoodfacts.org` (API v3.4) through `EphemeralWebSession`, redirects refused, 128 KB cap, 20 s deadline, consent re-checked inside. A no-tracking-wall pinned HTTP-client file (see [No-Tracking-Wall.md](No-Tracking-Wall.md) §3). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/OpenFoodFactsProduct.swift` | The pure half of the Open Food Facts lookup: `OpenFoodFactsBarcode` (GTIN validation, UPC-E expansion, OFF's code normalization), the bounded response classifier and product parser, and the mapping into a `.openFoodFacts` `FoodItem` / the naming screen's scan model. No network API named. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/OpenFoodFactsLookupCard.swift` | The lookup's consent gate (`OpenFoodFactsLookupAccess`), audited run (`OpenFoodFactsBarcodeLookup` — AI activity log at dispatch), `FernletStore.saveOpenFoodFactsFood`, and the not-found screen's one-tap-per-lookup card carrying the ODbL attribution. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/NutritionTargetsEditor.swift` | `NutritionTargetsEditor` — Settings card to edit macro targets (calories/protein/fat overridable, carbs shown as the live rebalancing residual); carries `WeightManagementDeficitRow` above Calories while Weight Management is the goal. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WeightManagementDeficitRow.swift` | `WeightManagementDeficitRow` + its view-free rules `WeightManagementDeficitControl` — the Weight Management calorie-deficit stepper on the Nutrition targets card (0–20% in 5% steps, 10% by default, owner 2026-09-24), shown only for that goal, disabled with the reason while a pinned calorie target outranks it. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletScoring/Scoring.swift` | Health scoring, goal weights, meal parsing, workout planning, workout suggestions, and date helpers. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletScoring/CompanionEmotionEngine.swift` | `CompanionEmotionEngine` — the pure emotion derivation and its explicit precedence table, `CompanionEmotionInputs` (+ `widgetSafe`, `isGentleDay`), `CompanionSleepWindow`, and the time-stable day timeline the widget renders from. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletScoring/PeriodScoringSignals.swift` | Abstract period-scoring vocabulary (`PeriodSignalStrength`, `PeriodPhaseSignal`, `PeriodScoringAdjustment`) consumed by the scoring engine. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/BarcodeScanner.swift` | Vision still-photo product-barcode detection (`BarcodePayloadDetecting` seam + `VisionBarcodeDetector`/`BarcodeScanner`) over EAN-13/EAN-8/UPC-E, the fallback for devices lacking live `DataScannerViewController`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/FoodImageClassifier.swift` | On-device meal-photo image classification behind the `FoodImageClassifying` seam (`VisionFoodImageClassifier` via `VNClassifyImageRequest`), returning raw taxonomy labels; the photo never leaves the device. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/WebScrapingKit/EphemeralWebSession.swift` | `EphemeralWebSession` — the single private-browsing `URLSession` (ephemeral + cookies/cache/credentials explicitly disabled) that both web importers and the Open Food Facts barcode client fetch through. The per-setting rationale, including which knobs are redundant under `.ephemeral` and why they are set anyway, lives here (see [No-Tracking-Wall.md](No-Tracking-Wall.md) §2a). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/WebScrapingKit/HTMLScraper.swift` | `HTMLScraper` — shared regex capture reads, HTML-entity decoding (`decodingNumericEntities:` preserves both importers' differing policies), and the body-to-plain-text pass; returns `nil` on an empty page so each caller throws its own error. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/WebScrapingKit/JSONLDScraper.swift` | `JSONLDScraper` — `application/ld+json` script extraction, `@type` reading, and the recursive `@graph`/`itemListElement` search that finds a schema.org `Product` or `Recipe`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FoodCatalog/CuratedNutrientSources.swift` | Hand-authored "good source" foods per tracked micronutrient — the F2 gap-filling nudge's payload, pinned to real bundled-catalog ids so the card can bind the food and offer "add it". |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GroceryListComposer.swift` | F3 app-target glue between the recipe stores and the pure `GroceryAggregation` engine: catalog resolution the engine refuses to do, F4 "cook for N" scaling, and web-import lines through `DataExportBuilder.recipeIngredientLines`. The list is one-shot share text; only the weekly plan persists. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GroceryPlannerView.swift` | The Phase A shopping-list builder: multi-select recipes with an optional per-recipe yield, preview the aggregated list, share as plain text. Nothing here persists. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/IngredientSubstitutionSheet.swift` | F4 substitution UI (§11.4): replace ONE structured ingredient. Applying forks a new recipe only on an explicit "Save as new recipe" tap — cancelling forks nothing and never mutates the source. That tap also remembers a replacement picked from below the top of the manual search list for words the person typed (ingredient-search round F9b, `RecipeSearchPick`) — never an AI suggestion, the seeded search or a search for the ingredient being replaced, and (fix round 1) never a row that does not say the words searched for while another row does: the list pools each sub-phrase's rows, so "Coconut" from the "coconut" block is a stand-in, not what "coconut oil" means. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CookingMode.swift` | `CookingModeAvailability` — the single rule deciding whether a recipe gets a "Cook" action (authored steps, structured ingredients, or a web import's free-text lines). UIKit-free. The mise en place lays a multipart recipe out part by part ("Made in 2 parts. Start with …") and the walker names the current part. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePartsDraft.swift` | The recipe editor's multipart builder logic with no SwiftUI (`RecipePartsDraft`: split, add/remove/move parts, per-part rows and steps, whole-recipe caps, collapse back to one part), and `RecipeEditorInputs` (stored rows → editor rows, shared by the one-part editor and every part). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePartsEditor.swift` | The editor's parts UI: `RecipePartsEditor` (one `RecipePartEditorCard` per part, "Add another part") plus the row and step editors now SHARED with the one-part editor (`RecipeIngredientRows`, `RecipeStepEditorCard`, `RecipeEditorActionLabel`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePartsViews.swift` | Read-side part grouping for the recipe detail, mise en place and the proximity review sheet: `RecipePartsLayout` (displayed/scaled rows grouped by id), `RecipePartHeader` ("Part 1 of 2", a VoiceOver heading), `RecipePartsIngredientList`, `RecipePartsStepList`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CookingLiveActivityController.swift` | App-target-only requester of the cooking Live Activity (starting one is the only thing solely the app process can do). Updates/ends go through the shared `CookingActivityBridge`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/BarcodeServingStepView.swift` | The quick serving-count step after a barcode scan, plus its device-local last-serving memory keyed by normalized GTIN — a `UserDefaults` sidecar deliberately kept off the synced/sealed blob path. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeWebImageAttemptMemory.swift` | Device-local `UserDefaults` sidecar recording which recipes' one automatic web-image download THIS device already attempted — the per-device half of the “one attempt per device, suppression syncs” contract (the synced half is `RecipeWebImport.webImageSuppressed`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FoodSearchCorrectionMemory.swift` | Research §26 fix 1.10's local correction memory: a device-local `UserDefaults` sidecar (never synced between devices, capped at 200) pairing the text a user searched in "Adjust meal" with the food they picked, republished into `FoodCatalog` so the same search self-heals thereafter. Since F9b each entry carries an origin (frozen `correction` / `recipePick`; a correction's stored bytes are unchanged): recipe picks publish into `FoodCatalog.setRecipeSearchPicks` (recipe surfaces only), an ordinary pick never overwrites a correction while a correction replaces a pick, and the cap spends picks before corrections. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipeSearchPick.swift` | Ingredient-search round F9b: when a pick in a recipe ingredient list teaches search — a row below the first, a real search, a finished last word (plural-aware; "chocolate ch" teaches nothing), and in the swap sheet not the seeded search or a search for the ingredient being replaced, and only a row that says the words the swap list searched for (its single sub-phrase words, either number) unless no shown row does (fix round 1) — and the recipe picks a save's rows carry (`ManualRecipeIngredientInput.pickedForSearch`). The recipe editor's tap only marks the row; the store's recipe saves write. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePortionGramsMemory.swift` | F4b's "grams in one" memory: a device-local `UserDefaults` sidecar (`fernlet.recipePortionGrams.v1`, never synced, capped at 200, oldest-out) of the grams a person gave for one of a food in place of a USDA typical size, written when a recipe is saved and offered under "Your size" next time; cleared by the wipe funnel. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/RecipePortionMenu.swift` | The SwiftUI half of F4b's per-food unit menu: `RecipePortionMenu` (a `Menu` holding one sectioned inline `Picker` over `RecipePortionPicker.Choices`), `RecipePortionGramsRow` (the "USDA typical size, estimate" / "Your size" badge and the editable grams for one), `RecipePortionAskGramsRow` (fix round 1, report §6.3 Rung E: "How many grams is one?" where nothing on the menu counts or a line's unit does not convert), `RecipeTypicalSizeEstimateNote` (the estimate note under a saved line on the recipe page and in cooking mode), and `RecipeHouseholdCaption` ("Counted as 2 eggs (100.6 g)", inflected with `^[…](inflect: true)`). The menu's VoiceOver value is the current choice. |

## AI Context And Providers

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/AIContextPayload.swift` | `AIContextPayload` marker protocol and concrete payload types (food-selection, meal-decomposition, etc.) defining the exact allowlisted fields that may enter each AI prompt. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/MemoryAgent.swift` | Gate routing Tier-2 memory into AI prompts through a destination allowlist, recency filter, confidence filter, and diagnostic-language post-classifier, returning a char-capped context string. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/AIAuditLog.swift` | `AIAuditEntry` metadata record plus the in-session `AIAuditLog` actor logging each AI call's payload kind, destination, included field names, and memory char count — metadata only, never persisted. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/FoundationWorkoutAdjustment.swift` | On-device workout adjuster: `WorkoutAdjustmentCandidateBuilder` builds the equipment/injury-filtered, ranked, capped candidate pool (never a hidden exercise) and `FoundationWorkoutAdjustmentModel` maps a natural-language request to substitutions within it via FoundationModels. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/AICapabilityTier.swift` | The minimum capability an AI task declares, plus its ordered `escalationLadder`. The router picks the *cheapest available* destination meeting the tier, never above device capability or the user's ceiling (Ladder §3.1). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/AIDeviceCapability.swift` | Snapshot of which AI rungs the *device* can physically reach, independent of user settings; obtained through the `AIDeviceCapabilityProviding` seam so tests can inject a fixed value. `.webNutritionLookup` always reports false by design (settings-gated web path, not an LLM rung). Since 2026-10-16 the seam also names `OnDeviceModelAvailability` (available, not eligible, Apple Intelligence off, not ready, unnamed), display only; a capability-only provider answers available or unnamed. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/AICallQuota.swift` | The daily AI-call budget with day-key rollover (Ladder §3.2). A pure value the *caller* stores device-locally — the derived `.sleepy`/`.resting` states must never reach synced `FernletSettings`, or one device's usage would throttle another. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/FernletModelRouter.swift` | Capability-capped route resolution and its `AIRouteResolution`/fallback-reason vocabulary, letting deferred tasks tell a transient budget fallback from a persistent one before spending a retry. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIContext/FernletAIGate.swift` | The single routing entry point every AI call site funnels through — router + stored user intent + device-local quota in one value. `dispatch`/`resolveRoute` are the only places the daily quota is charged. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/SystemLanguageModelCapabilityProvider.swift` | Production `AIDeviceCapabilityProviding`: wraps `SystemLanguageModel.default.availability`, and maps its unavailable reason onto `OnDeviceModelAvailability` for the Settings "Today" row. The PCC and BYOK rungs report false here — their symbols are iOS 27 APIs. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/FoundationIngredientSubstitution.swift` | On-device stage for F4 substitution: the model contributes world knowledge only (substitute food *names* plus an optional reason) — never a quantity, macro, or binding. Code rebinds each name through the local catalog. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AIProviders/FoundationJournalSummary.swift` | On-device stage for the journal → Core Memory summary: `light` tier (never leaves the device), ambient (yields to the sleepy band), fed only the entry via `JournalSummaryPayload`; the reply must pass `JournalMemorySummaryPolicy` or it is audited as a fallback and dropped. No retry by design. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/JournalMemorySummarizer.swift` | `JournalMemorySummarizing`, the seam `FernletStore` asks for a journal memory's summary, and its production conformer over the walled stage; a test double stands in because the model never runs in the simulator. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletAIComposition.swift` | Composition-root factory for the AI provider seam. Lives in its own file on purpose: naming `SystemLanguageModel…` matches the S3 grep-wall's AI-facing marker, keeping the type out of `FernletStore.swift`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/AICallQuotaStore.swift` | The device-local, non-synced daily AI-call counter backed by `UserDefaults`. Deliberately app-target-only: the walled AI module reaches it solely through the `AICallQuotaStore` protocol declared in `AIContext`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/AIStatusPresentation.swift` | What Settings' AI card says today: the "Today" value and the voice line from the effective `AIStatus` and the device's `OnDeviceModelAvailability`. The person's "Off" wins; otherwise a device that cannot run the on-device model says why ("Not available on this iPhone", "Turn on Apple Intelligence", "Getting ready"), where the effective status alone said "Ready" forever on such a device (bug confirmation 2026-10-15, food-ai §7). Display only; read through `FernletStore.aiStatusPresentation`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FileAIAuditLogStore.swift` | Device-local, non-synced persistence sink for the AI audit log (Ladder §7.2) — one JSON file in Application Support, reached only through the `AIAuditLogPersisting` protocol. |

## Health And Launch Services

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/HealthKitGateway/HealthKitService.swift` | HealthKit service protocol and implementation, capability metadata, authorization snapshots, body/activity/cycle/mindfulness context, and authorization view model. Since 2026-09-23 also THE write gate: every write into Apple Health passes `requireWriteSharing(_:)` (master switch + that kind's switch) and reaches the store only through `save(_:)` or the `HealthKitStoreControlling.saveWorkout` seam, where the workout builder now lives. Since 2026-10-20 also `HealthKitLiveHeartRateSource`: guided cardio's iPhone workout session (heart rate only, gated on workout sharing, its workout discarded). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/HealthKitGateway/WorkoutHealthKitSync.swift` | HealthKit workout import pipeline; reads `HKWorkout` samples and upserts matching `Workout` records via a `WorkoutSyncContext`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/HealthSyncCoordinator.swift` | `HealthSyncCoordinator` (`HealthSyncContext`) — HealthKit ingestion (daily-context merge, derived sleep, workout import/backfill/observe) extracted from `FernletStore`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/CoreDataHealthKitCacheCleaner.swift` | `CoreDataHealthKitCacheCleaner` — concrete `HealthKitCacheClearing` that scrubs HealthKit values an older build left in the synced rows / blob and empties the device-local HealthKit residue cache, fail-closed so opt-out never silently "succeeds"; its capture mode is the one-time launch scrub. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DeviceHealthResidueStore.swift` | `FileDeviceHealthResidueStore` (the device-local, backup-excluded, bounded JSON cache of this device's HealthKit readings; one `production` instance) and `InMemoryDeviceHealthResidueStore` (the synchronous initializer's hermetic default). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DeviceDaySummaryStore.swift` | `FileDeviceDaySummaryStore` (the device-local, backup-excluded, bounded JSON file of this device's AI day summaries — `Application Support/FernletDeviceDaySummaries`, one `production` instance, cleared by "Delete everything", untouched by the HealthKit opt-out) and `InMemoryDeviceDaySummaryStore` (the synchronous initializer's hermetic default). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore+HealthBodyProfile.swift` | `FernletStore` extension: this device's HealthKit body-profile import (age, sex, height, weight) recorded device-locally instead of into the synced `settings.userProfile` (2026-09-23), `effectiveUserProfile`/`effectiveSettings`, the profile-screen edit split (only changed fields reach settings), the opt-out reload, and the `DeviceHealthBodyProfile(imported:)` bridge from the gateway type. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore+ProximityDebugTools.swift` | `FernletStore.proximityDebugToolsEnabled`: the Release clamp on the synced `showProximityDebugTools` setting (the stored value in DEBUG, always false in Release, where no switch exists to turn a synced `true` off). Gates the Friends tab's raw discovery diagnostic, the recipe-share "Connection details" log, the Force override and the Connection Inspector entry. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore+RecipeShare.swift` | `FernletStore` extension (2026-09-30): `recipeShareDraft(for:)` (the one draft every Share-screen entry point builds), `recipeShareText(for:includesNotes:)` (through `RecipeShareText`, replacing the two old builders) and `recipeMessagesCardOffer(for:includesNotes:)` with its pure `messagesCardOffer` — `ready` with the packet only when the card's URL fits, else `tooLarge`, `webImported` (the pinned no-ingredients defect is not widened) or `unavailable` (`RecipeMessagesCardOffer`). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore+HealthKitResidue.swift` | `FernletStore` extension: records today's HealthKit residue at the ingestion points, installs the Core Data repository's Tier-2 refresher that puts this device's residue back into Tier-2's window (2026-10-18), and the once-per-device launch scrub of HealthKit values out of rows older builds synced. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutHealthAccessOffer.swift` | `WorkoutHealthAccessOffer` + `WorkoutHealthAccessOutcome` — the one contextual Apple Health ask at the first workout logged or started (onboarding's "Asked the first time you log a workout…"): mirrors "Give access" (master + workout switches, prompt, ledger), restores the switches on a decline, and never asks twice, after an explicit off in Settings, or when HealthKit would show no sheet. Its durable fact `fernlet.healthkit.workoutAccessOfferResolved` lives in the store's sensitive-surface suite and is cleared by "delete everything". |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/GuidedRunDuration.swift` | `GuidedRunDuration` — the length a finished guided run logs: wall clock from the run's `startedAt` to its `finishedAt` (no pause exists to subtract), whole minutes in `Workout.duration` like the Log sheet, at least 1, capped at 3 hours with a note in the row's notes; the Apple Health copy follows from the row (ends at the finish, starts `duration` earlier). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/HealthAccessGrant.swift` | `HealthAccessGrant.requestInContext` — asking for one kind of Health data where a feature first needs it, with the effect Settings' "Give access" has: the kind's Fernlet switch goes on, the prompt runs, the ask is ledgered, and a declined prompt puts the switch back. Never turns the master switch on. Exists because the gateway's write gate (2026-09-23) reads that switch. The period sheet and Cycle page no longer call it (owner question Q4); kept so the ask can be reinstated in one call. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/WorkoutTombstoneStore.swift` | `WorkoutTombstoneStore` — persisted capped FIFO ring of removed workout ids so a resurrected orphan HealthKit sample is deleted-and-skipped rather than re-imported. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/PrivateHealthStore/CyclePredictionEngine.swift` | Statistical cycle prediction engine using historical period data to forecast next period start, flow patterns, confidence level, and predicted flow days. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/LaunchPreparationService.swift` | Launch preparation state and photo wall seed loading. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/StartupTiming.swift` | `os_signpost`-based startup timing probes for measuring and instrumenting app launch phases. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/NotificationService.swift` | Thin `UNUserNotificationCenter` wrapper for the opt-in gentle daily check-in local reminder (authorization, status check, scheduling), the mesh session-message ping, and the guided workout's in-session rest-end / hold-end cue for a phone in the background (`replaceWorkoutCues`, never prompts, cancelled when the run ends). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/WeatherKitService.swift` | Opt-in coarse-location WeatherKit service producing tiny derived snapshots (`WeatherComfort` walk-friendliness, `AmbientSky` sky bucket) for mood-recovery prompts and Home ambience; degrades to nil, leaking no raw data. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/AppServices/WeatherRequestBudget.swift` | `WeatherRequestBudget` (ten WeatherKit calls per local day, rolled over at midnight) and `WeatherRequestBudgetStore` (the one `fernlet.weather.requestBudget` UserDefaults pair, cleared by delete-everything) — the cap `WeatherKitService` spends before every call. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/FernletDate.swift` | Layer-0 date helpers (`FernletDate`): canonical `yyyy-MM-dd` day-key formatting, enumeration, and display. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/MonotonicClock.swift` | `MonotonicClock` protocol plus sleep-counting `SystemMonotonicClock` (`mach_continuous_time`) for the store-ban clock. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/FernletAuditLog.swift` | Privacy audit-log facade (`FernletAuditLog`) emitting os_log events with a test-observable capture-handler registry. ProximityKit's lines arrive through FernletConnections' `FernletAuditBridge`, the sink `FernletApp.init` installs (plan step A0.2.10). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/PersistenceFailureAudit.swift` | `PersistenceFailureAudit`: the one seam every ENVIRONMENTAL persistence failure records through — a Core Data fetch/save/delete, a file write/remove, or the payload encode that feeds one. Replaced the `assertionFailure`-in-`catch` those sites carried (a DEBUG trap on a locked-device write). Records the frozen dotted token plus the error's `NSError` domain and code only — never `localizedDescription` or `userInfo`. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/BackupExclusion.swift` | Layer-0 helper (`BackupExclusion`) toggling `isExcludedFromBackupKey` across a Core Data store file and its sidecars. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletFoundation/FernletFoundation.swift` | Overview/placeholder source standing up the layer-0 `FernletFoundation` module. |

## Social And Mesh Networking

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/Feasibility/NetworkMeshFeasibilityProbe.swift` | DEBUG-only Bonjour/QUIC probe with a Simulator infrastructure lane and a physical-device peer-to-peer/background gate; verifies the signed TLS-channel binding, its introduction signed and checked under `MeshProbeConstants.channelIntroductionPurpose`, a host signature label minted with ProximityKit's `featureSignature(_:framing:)`, the twin of its FernletCrypto entry (plan step A1.0). Since the re-pin to ProximityKit 0.1.1 (2026-10-17) its listener sets no finite new-connection limit, which the framework spends one connection at a time and never refunds (0.1.1's fix to the radios); its one inbound tunnel and `maxConnections` bound it, and `MeshNetworkFeasibilityTests` pins the listener's limit. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/Feasibility/MeshRejectionMatrixHarness.swift` | DEBUG-only launch hooks for the runbook's Lane C (two Simulators, the PRODUCTION mesh over QUIC): `MeshMatrixDebugOptions` reads `FERNLET_MESH_MATRIX*` once per process, and the harness seeds a memory-only `MeshDescriptor` roster, echoes this device's signing identity, and starts the radios so each named `MeshIntroductionRejection` can be observed on a real radio. Off when absent; compiled out in release. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/Feasibility/MeshFlowDriver.swift` | DEBUG-only Lane C app-flow driver: `FERNLET_MESH_FLOWS=<csv>` commits each device's own slot at either proximity gate and then drives chat, photos and the clothing shop through the same public entry points the UI uses, echoing both sides' observations under `[mesh-flow]`. Off when absent; compiled out in release. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/DisposableCameraView.swift` | Active Friends-session disposable-camera UI: `CameraCaptureController` (`@Observable`, queued `AVCaptureSession` lifecycle, rotation handling, and arm/wind gate), `CameraPreviewView`, and the full-screen camera. The session-info sheet exposes the 10-shot film count, open/closed access, mesh rename, participant options, friend-of-friend labels, diagnostics, removal requests, and end-session flow; admission requests and mesh errors are presented as popups. The Develop review offers the photo ids snapshotted at Develop and renders/answers them wherever the manager holds them (`photosAwaitingAnswer(among:)`, `finishSessionPhotos(keeping:of:)`), so door 3's give-up moving the roll under the open review cannot void the person's answer; it reports whether any sheet or alert of its own is up to `FriendsView` (`presentsOwnSheet`). Since 2026-09-30 the review's tiles load held photos through `reviewThumbnailData(for:)`, the answer is read before anything else happens (an unapplied one keeps the sheet up), and the camera-roll copy is the opt-in toggle, exported after the answer over `keptOnWall` from the wall. Since session photos U3 it reports its Develop review to `SessionPhotoReviewCoordinator.cameraDevelopReviewUp` from its presentation flag and clears it on the camera's AND the review sheet's disappear (the gate reads it ANDed with `isInSession`, I20), raises the coordinator's answer leg around its finish-and-leave (`beginAnswer()` / `defer { endAnswer() }`, I21), waits for a failed export's alert on the coordinator's `developSaveFailureAcknowledgement` (not a `@State` object: U3 fix round 1, so a termination or swipe-down never strands that leg), and stops its capture session while the overlay shows (`handleOverlayChange`), restarting only over a live session. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/JoinPromptSheet.swift` | `JoinPromptSheet` — the shared "someone wants to join" confirmation for both closed-group gatekeepers: mesh admission requests (presented by the disposable-camera session flow) and Group Activity join requests (presented by `ActivitiesView`). Generic over the request payload via a display-name extractor closure (callers pass the name through `PeerNameDisplay`; no fingerprint since 2026-09-29); swipe-to-dismiss declines everything still pending (fail-closed). Replaced the former `MeshAdmissionPromptSheet.swift` and `ActivityJoinPromptSheet.swift`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FriendListView.swift` | Friend list screen for browsing trusted proximity peers with all/friends/blocked filters and block management. Names render through `PeerNameDisplay` ("Someone you met" for a friend kept before their name arrived); the detail card's collapsed "Safety code" is the one place the app still shows a fingerprint. Report runs the shared `peerReportFlow` (2026-10-15), which adds the developer-contact note after a report. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/ActivitiesView.swift` | `ActivitiesView` — Group Activities screen (host form / nearby invites / hosting roster / joined states) off the Friends tab, driven by `ProximityActivityManager`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SessionChatPanel.swift` | `SessionChatPanel` — live-session chat panel over `MeshNetworkManager`: the memory-only transcript (cleared at session end) plus, since **P6 item 4**, an INLINE notice for a send that did not stage, shown at the TOP of the compose bar (above the text field, below the transcript — five docs said "beneath", corrected at item 4's fix review) with the draft kept. Reads `sendTempMessage(_:)`'s returned `MeshTextSendOutcome` rather than `routedShareRefusal`, whose one consumer is a `.alert` on the `DisposableCameraView` this panel is presented over. Since 2026-10-15 (App Review guideline 1.2): touch and hold another person's message — never your own — for **Report…** (also the row's VoiceOver action), which opens `peerReportFlow` and files through `onReport`; and the send filter's `.filtered` refusal is said in the same inline notice, draft kept. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/SafetyReportingView.swift` | `SafetyReportingView` — always-available report/block explainer and no-tolerance policy for shared shop items, people, and (2026-10-15) session messages and photos, plus the chat's send filter (App Store UGC compliance, guideline 1.2). Its `contactURL` is the one developer contact route, which the report flow offers too. Since 2026-10-16 it opens on `CrisisSupportCard`, because Settings search sends "crisis", "help", "988", "suicide", "hotline" and "first aid" here. Its paragraphs are `LocalizedStringKey` since 2026-10-15 (they were `String` and reached no catalog). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/PeerReportFlow.swift` | **App Review guideline 1.2 (2026-10-15)**: the app's ONE people-report flow — `PeerReportSubject` (who a report names and what for: a person, a session message by a ≤32-character excerpt, or a photo; its factories refuse this device's own message and photo) and `View.peerReportFlow(_:onReport:)`, the reason sheet (one destructive button per `ReportReason`, an alert for iOS 26's dialog reason) and then a "Reported" note offering the developer contact route. Used by Friends & Blocks, the chat panel, both photo reviews and the wall viewer; it files nothing itself. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/FernletStore+SessionReports.swift` | **App Review guideline 1.2 (2026-10-15)**: `reportableSenderKey(of:)` (a photo's verified sender key, else the vault's, never this device's), `reportSessionPeer(_:reason:)` (the session's report funnel: `reportProximityPeer` — the vault's report-and-block on this device, no second store — then the chat's `refreshVisibleTranscript()`), and `reportWallPhoto(_:reason:)` (the funnel plus `MeshSessionPhotos.removeReportedPhoto`). |

## Proximity

ProximityKit's own sources (the radios, identity, sealing, the session coordinator, the routed mesh engine, the presence engine and the pair session) are its own repository's since ProximityKit plan step A1.3, indexed there: Fernlet depends on [bowman-mw/proximitykit](https://github.com/bowman-mw/proximitykit) at exactly `0.1.1` (Dependency, below). The rows here are this repository's files over it: FernletConnections, FernletSocial, FernletProximityUI and the app.

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/PayloadType.swift` | Fernlet's payload vocabulary: `PayloadType` (every payload token, by reverse-DNS raw value) and `ProximityCapability` (every capability token a device advertises). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/FriendPhotoPayloads.swift` | Friend-photo payload, session metadata, manifest, and missing-photo request wire models. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/Audit/ConnectionInspector.swift` | Live and historical proximity session logging, ranging mode/distance samples, RTT samples, event subsampling, and session lifecycle recording (`ProximityInspectorRecording`). Converts ProximityKit's report types into `ConnectionSessionLog`'s own, field for field and case for case (its session mode through `FernletConnections`' `ProximityMode.init(_:)`), and applies its transport events (the first connected stamp kept, the latest 50 round trips). |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletDomainModel/ConnectionSessionLog.swift` | Structured session log model capturing peer info, ranging state, transport counters, envelope records, and error events. Keeps its own `Role` and `RangingMode`, copies of ProximityKit's enums with the same raw values, so the persisted JSON names no ProximityKit type. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/UI/ConnectionInspectorView.swift` | On-demand live connection inspector UI for real-time proximity diagnostics, distance, RTT, and fallback status. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/UI/ConnectionInspectorHistoryView.swift` | View for browsing and reviewing historical proximity connection session logs. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletConnections/PeerNameDisplay+Placeholders.swift` | The Fernlet app's half of the name display, a `nonisolated extension` of ProximityKit's `PeerNameDisplay`: `Placeholder` (`.nearby` "Someone nearby" for the connect path, `.met` "Someone you met" for the keep-as-friend rows and Friends & Blocks), `shown(_:fingerprint:placeholder:in:)` (the filtered name or the placeholder), `firstName(_:fingerprint:placeholder:in:)` (the name's first word for warm hearts copy, or the WHOLE placeholder; `FernletSocial`'s `PresenceManager.firstName(of:in:)` delegates to it) and `text(for:)` (the placeholder alone, from `FernletConnectionsCopy.Peer`). Every caller passes `in: .fernlet` or its namespace; display only, never persisted or sent. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletConnections/FernletConnectionsCopy.swift` | `FernletConnectionsCopy` — the module's copy vault (the `FernletUICopy`/`FernletSocialCopy` model): `Peer.someoneNearby` and `Peer.someoneYouMet`, computed `String(localized:defaultValue:bundle:comment:)` lookups with `bundle: .module` against the module's own `Localizable.xcstrings`, keys `proximity.peer.*` (a key is a token, so they keep that spelling). `nonisolated`, as the name display is. |
| `Fernlet/FernletKit/Sources/FernletProximityUI/KeepFriendsPromptSheet.swift` | `KeepFriendsSection` + prompt sheet — session-end one-sided "keep as a friend?" affordance that mints a local-only trust-vault record (peer never notified). |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/UI/ProximityRecipeShareSheet.swift` | `ProximityRecipeShareSheet` + `ProximityRecipeShareDraft` — the recipe Share screen: a nearby Fernlet over the proximity channel, and (since 2026-09-30) the "Share outside Fernlet" card below it (`RecipeShareElsewhereCard`); its "Include notes" switch sits above both cards and covers every route, while "Include picture" stays with nearby. The draft carries the recipe itself, so the text and the Messages card follow the switch. When a share ends it cross-fades to `RecipeShareConfirmationPanel` (latched from the manager's `lastShareOutcome`, announced, success/error haptic) and stays until Done instead of auto-dismissing; a successful send moves radio custody to a hand back that, 1.4 s after the send, runs the same gated stop-and-restart its disappearance would, so an early Done or swipe-down never stops the radio before the sent frame drains. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/UI/RecipeShareElsewhereCard.swift` | `RecipeShareElsewhereCard` + `ComposedRecipeCard` (2026-09-30, the owner's "it pastes the raw json data" report) — "Send in Messages" (the iMessage app's own recipe card in a Messages draft, or a one-line note in its place: Messages not set up, too long for a card, or saved from a web page) and "Share as text" (`RecipeShareText` through `RecipeShareTextItemSource` in the system share sheet, built at the tap). 44-pt rows, decorative glyphs, a "Sent in Messages." / failure line that VoiceOver hears; a cancelled draft says nothing. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/UI/RecipeShareConfirmation.swift` | The recipe share confirmation: `RecipeShareConfirmation` (pure mapping from ProximityKit's frozen `ProximityPairSendOutcome` to localized headline / detail / VoiceOver announcement — says "sent", never "delivered", and keeps "may have it" apart from "nothing was sent"), `RecipeShareOutcomeLatch` (the sheet latches only the outcome of a share it began, once, for the tapped row, so a cancelled share raises nothing), `RecipeShareConfirmationPanel` (glyph, header-trait heading, detail, Try again / Done pills; scrolls at AX5), and `RecipeShareRadioCustody` + `RecipeShareRadioHandBack` (the post-send drain rule: who stops the radio, when, and whether passive listening restarts). Pinned by `RecipeShareConfirmationTests`. |
| `Fernlet/App/Fernlet/Proximity/UI/ProximityRecipeShareReviewSheet.swift` | `ProximityRecipeShareReviewSheet` — review an incoming proximity-shared recipe (title, servings, macros) before saving it. |
